experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview/reviews/lit_5.md:495:- A 语义/VLM teacher: 已完成,见 experiments/paper_materials/newinfo_semantic_teacher-codex.md。结论是遮挡语义 teacher 对 SOLIDER 强主干整体偏负,FLaN-Net/π-VL/AG-ReID/LVLM-ReID/RMPSNet 等发表遮挡成绩多低于或接近现有强基线; 唯一值得先探的是属性 teacher 的可靠性噪声屏蔽,其次是 teacher 只用于属性级样本组织,两者都必须先做零训练探针。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:1:# 实验 exp359: LM-ReID (Lattice-Marginalized ReID)
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:3:> 后 PRCV「换问题」阶段第一个 GO 的 method 候选（9 个零训练 cheap-kill 全死后）。零训练 kill-switch 已 GO（d8 lattice, agent a68e6），method-design codex 判 **CCF-B 7/10**（训练版超 frozen ensemble → 8/10）。本实验做训练版，证明 lattice-marginalization 是 **method 不是 ensemble trick**。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:4:> 设计来源：`experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt`（完整 codex 设计）+ `cvpb_lattice_result.md`（kill-switch 数据）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:8:低分辨率 ReID 的传统视角：LR = 模糊/缺细节，解法 = SR / resolution-invariant feature。**我们重定义**：一部分 LR 失败不是"缺信息"，而是 **采样格点不确定性（sampling-lattice uncertainty）**——同一个 HR 身份在不同合法的 LR 采样格点（sub-pixel phase / bbox alignment / downsample kernel）下，落到不同 embedding 区域，导致 rank-1 身份翻转。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:11:frozen exp260b Market，K=9 lattice variants ensemble，HR gallery / LR query：
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:20:两个生死控制都过：**vs 普通 TTA**（同 K 同 MaxSim，lattice 比 TTA 多 +3.04，证 lattice-specific 非 TTA 换名；lat-MaxSim 46.9 > tta 43.8 > single 42.6，无"输给 trivial baseline"问题）；**vs #false-in-topk**（phase-var partial +0.19/+0.21 存活）。机制：phase-var 是 TTA-var 的 **7.5×**；74.9% h=16 query 跨 lattice 翻转 rank-1，随 h 升单调衰减到 1.2%（h=48 机制消失 = 低分辨率专用现象）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:23:phase-var 作 per-query 失败预测器**不干净**（控 LR-severity 后 partial 塌到 ≈0，与 per-image LR 失真共线）。**GO 靠的是 interventional 结果**（ensemble +4.2 / LAT−TTA +3.0 是直接测量）。故事写成 "lattice sensitivity 是 **mechanism-level nuisance**, 不是 standalone failure predictor"，方法是对所有 severe-LR query 做 marginalization（不是预测哪张失败）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:27:训练一个 lattice-marginalized embedding（对 lattice variants 身份稳定）+ 推理 K-marginalization，在 h=16 上比 frozen lattice ensemble **再高 +0.8~2.0 mAP** → 证明它学到了 lattice-invariance（是 method），不是 ensemble trick。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:33:2. fine-tune：HR train image 在线生成 LR lattice variants `x^l = U(D_l(x))`，l ∈ {sub-pixel phase, bbox jitter, downsample kernel}。每图每 iter 采样 M=2-4 variants，eval 用 K=9。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:36:### LM-ReID loss
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:40:L_marg = -log[ mean_l p^l[y] ] + Triplet(z^μ, y)                 # marginal likelihood（主贡献）
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:42:L_adv  = GRL-CE(Dφ(z^l), lattice_label_l)                        # 弱：去掉 embedding 中可预测 lattice label
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:47:### 推理 K-marginalization
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:58:- h=32：允许 marginal 不负。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:60:失败最可能原因：训练版只 ≈ frozen ensemble（没学到额外 lattice-invariance）→ 沦为 test-time ensemble trick，不成方法稿。备选投稿角度：同等 mAP 下 K 从 9 降到 3 或 single inference 保留大部分收益。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:66:- **frozen lattice ensemble**（零训练 K=9，= kill-switch 的 +4.23，这是训练版必须超过的硬线）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:69:消融：marg only / marg+cons / marg+cons+adv；τ sweep；K=1/3/5/9 曲线；phase-only vs +bbox+kernel。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:73:- 合成：Market/MSMT，gallery HR，query LR h=16/24/32，canonical LR single baseline，K=9（3×3 phase 主，bbox/kernel ablation，不无限扩 K）。所有 TTA 对照 **K-matched**。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:75:- 新指标：PRF@1（phase rank-flip rate）、Flip Entropy、LEG（lattice ensemble gain）、LOTG（lattice-over-TTA gain）、query ΔAP。按 h 分报 + paired bootstrap 95% CI + K=1/3/5/9 曲线 + compute cost。PRF 随 h（74.9%→31%→10%→1.2%）是强故事线。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:79:- **VPFA**（2510.00936，最近邻）：CR-ReID = feature-space resolution direction，Vector Panning LR→pseudo-HR。hidden variable 是 resolution gap/feature direction，**不是 sampling lattice，不做 lattice marginalization**。必须正面对比。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:80:- LRAR（2207.13037）：resolution-adaptive representation，占"resolution adaptive"词 → 我们 novelty 写 sampling-lattice uncertainty。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:81:- RFD（2109.07871）：multi-res gallery distillation，占"resolution-invariant distillation"，没占 lattice intervention。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:82:- BlurPool（1904.11486）：anti-aliasing/shift-invariance 已老 → **不能说首次发现 aliasing 影响**。**能声称**：首次在 LR person ReID 把采样格点作隐藏变量 + rank-level intervention 证明 + lattice-specific marginalization 解决检索身份翻转。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:83:- FlipReID（2105.05639）：ReID 常用 flip-mean TTA → **必须反复强调非 TTA 换名**（同 K vs-TTA 控制，lattice 多 +3.04）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:87:operating point 低（只在 h≤24 强 +3-4 mAP，h=32 marginal）→ 主动收窄定位 **"severe low-resolution / cross-resolution ReID under sampling-lattice uncertainty"**，不写成通用 ReID 鲁棒性稿。h=32 marginal 反而支持机制（分辨率升高 → lattice uncertainty 消退）。真正风险 = 训练版打不过 frozen ensemble，那就只是 ensemble trick。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:92:2. 实现 LM-ReID（插件式，config 开关，lattice aug 复用 `cvpb_lattice_killswitch.py` 的 LR 生成）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:96:6. eval（**test.py 不用 train.py**）：LM-ReID(K marg) vs single / TTA / frozen-ensemble，h=16/24/32 分报，LEG/LOTG/PRF。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview/reviews/lit_13.md:176:- A 语义/VLM teacher: 已完成,见 experiments/paper_materials/newinfo_semantic_teacher-codex.md。结论是遮挡语义 teacher 对 SOLIDER 强主干整体偏负,FLaN-Net/π-VL/AG-ReID/LVLM-ReID/RMPSNet 等发表遮挡成绩多低于或接近现有强基线; 唯一值得先探的是属性 teacher 的可靠性噪声屏蔽,其次是 teacher 只用于属性级样本组织,两者都必须先做零训练探针。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview/reviews/lit_13.md:2352:with low resolution are difficult to localize fine-grained targets. Accordingly, DHGN captures finer-grained temporal
./experiments/exp323/design.md:35:- 失败最可能原因：(1) 低分辨率监控行人图上 MLLM 判定噪声大，A/B 都烂；(2) pose grounding 不改变判定（MLLM 不听 grounding 或本就用可见部位）；(3) 增益均匀分布（→ 通用重排，撞红线#6）。任一即砍，转 exp324。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_lsrc.md:1:# Codex Review — exp359-LSRC
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_lsrc.md:6:**审查对象**: `cvpb_lm_reid_train.py` LSRC loss（loss_mode=='lsrc'）+ args
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_lsrc.md:10:- **Critical**：代码默认 `lam_marg=1.0 / lam_cons=0.2`，`loss_mode==lsrc` 只追加 LSRC，不显式传 `--lam_marg 0 --lam_cons 0` 就混入已证伪的 L_marg/L_cons，污染 LSRC 结论。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_lsrc.md:11:- **High**：train/test marginalization 不对齐。训练 `sim4` 是双边 M×M LR-lattice bag-to-bag；test 是 query-LR-K变体 × gallery-HR-单图。对称训练给正样本 gallery-side oracle、给负样本 test 不存在的 gallery-side worst-pair 约束。建议非对称 `LR-query set → gallery single`，或至少加该对照。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_lsrc.md:13:- **Medium-2**：LSRC 机制上确与 consistency（非拉均值）/ L_marg（非分类头 posterior）有本质区别，但默认混旧项导致无法隔离证明区别。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_lsrc.md:18:没见"LR-ReID 把 sampling lattice 明确当 hidden variable 做 lattice decision marginalization"的直接先例。set contrastive/logsumexp bag score 本身不新（SupCon/MIL-NCE/set-ReID），novelty 只能落在 **sampling-lattice hidden variable + retrieval decision marginalization** 问题定义。不能声称发现 aliasing 本身（anti-aliasing 强先例）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_lsrc.md:23:2. **High** — 已加 `--lsrc_asym`（query M 变体 set × gallery slot-0 单图，对齐 test）。**策略**：对称版作便宜 go/no-go（acc 0.999 健康，~50min 跑完 eval）；对称 eval 涨 → **重跑 codex 审 asym diff → approve → 跑 asym 作主结果**；对称 eval 平 → 训练端这条基本凉，转 BLC / codex 找更多。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_v2_final.txt:14:- Medium-4 resolved: [358](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:358), [359](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:359), [365](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:365). Triplet is now per lattice slot over `[B,D]` with labels `y`, then averaged over `M`.
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview/reviews/lit_18.md:587:- A 语义/VLM teacher: 已完成,见 experiments/paper_materials/newinfo_semantic_teacher-codex.md。结论是遮挡语义 teacher 对 SOLIDER 强主干整体偏负,FLaN-Net/π-VL/AG-ReID/LVLM-ReID/RMPSNet 等发表遮挡成绩多低于或接近现有强基线; 唯一值得先探的是属性 teacher 的可靠性噪声屏蔽,其次是 teacher 只用于属性级样本组织,两者都必须先做零训练探针。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview/reviews/lit_18.md:980:et al., 2020), and battery life constraints. Both ground-based and UAVbased person ReID have similar limitations, such as low resolution (Xu
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_blc.md:1:# 实验 exp359-BLC: Bbox-Lattice Canonicalizer（训练端 input-level 备选）
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_blc.md:3:> **状态**：备选 design（2026-06-26 预写）。仅当 LSRC（对称 + 非对称）都 go/no-go 失败时启动。预写以备无缝接 + 体现"实验前先写 design"纪律。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_blc.md:7:- LSRC 走 backbone-loss 路线。codex train2_input 收敛到 **BLC（input-level，8/10→market 受限 6.5）**作互补/备选。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_blc.md:8:- **最对症的证据**：LM-S4 因子消融显示 **bbox 检测框不确定性主导（+2.84）**，远超 phase/kernel。→ 直接 canonicalize bbox 的 refiner 比改 resize kernel 更打中主因。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_blc.md:13:训一个 tiny crop refiner，把带 bbox/phase 扰动的 crop 重采样成 canonical crop，**削弱 bbox 主因子**，剩下的 phase/kernel/残差留给 test-time K=9 decision marginalization。目标**不是**让 K 个 lattice 特征一致（那是已证伪的 consistency），是把错误 crop 校正到更可靠的 person support。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_blc.md:19:  - `δ_z` 来自自生成的 bbox/phase lattice → 几何监督干净（压偏移参数，非身份 embedding）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_blc.md:22:- **⚠️ market 约束（codex 警告）**：market 图是已裁好的人框，无原图上下文 → 要先 **pad crops 人工制造 bbox 不确定性**，否则 refiner 没有可校正的 support 偏移。这是 BLC 在 market 上先天降到 6.5 的原因。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_blc.md:26:- 假设成立：offset MAE `<0.35` LR px，bbox-axis feature spread 降 ≥20%（identity margin 不降），K=1 mAP `+0.8` 或 K=9 在 lattice-marg 上再 `+0.3~0.5`。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_blc.md:31:- baseline：no-LM-loss（lattice 79.90 / single ~76.9）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_blc.md:37:冻 no-LM-loss backbone 只训 canonicalizer，h=12/16 bbox-only lattice：
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_blc.md:45:压几何偏移参数（非身份 embedding）→ 非 consistency；几何监督来自自生成 lattice（干净）→ 非弱监督瞎学；test 仍保留 K=9 marginalization → canonicalizer 只削 bbox 主因子，不替代边缘化。

experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:23:- **[epoch 5-7 收敛漂亮]** warmup 结束 cosine 段：**L 3.3→1.66，acc 0.888→0.979，cons loss 0.035→0.020（模型在变 lattice-invariant，机制在工作）**，marg 1.14→0.28。无崩溃。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:33:| frozen lattice ensemble | 46.87 | 零训练上界 |
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:34:| **LM-ReID single** | **69.96** | +27.3（"在 LR 上训"=标准 CR-ReID，**非我们创新**） |
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:35:| **LM-ReID lattice-mean** | **72.60** | +2.63 over single（lattice-marg，**真创新**） |
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:36:| LM-ReID ordinary-TTA | 69.09 | −0.87 |
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:37:| **LATTICE−TTA** | | **+3.504**（lattice-specific，干净 beat trivial TTA） |
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:39:h=24：single 78.67 → lattice 79.67（+1.00），LATTICE−TTA +0.978。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:41:**诚实分解**：①+27 大头是 LR fine-tune（标准），②lattice-marg 真贡献 +2.63 但 robust beat TTA +3.504（frozen +3.04 / trained +3.504 都成立）。**HR-gallery 退化担心是多虑**（SANITY 掉但 LR mAP 高=模型学了 LR↔HR 对应，牺牲 HR-HR 换 LR-HR 正是 CR-ReID 目标，已纠正误判）。codex 过线被远超**但大头是 LR fine-tune 不是 lattice 机制** → 真判据靠 ablation。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:45:- **no-LM-loss ablation**（PID 1347672）：M=3 保留 LR 增强，关 L_marg/L_cons（lam_marg=0 lam_cons=0），只 L_id。隔离 consistency 训练独立价值。out exp359_abl_noLMloss，等待 bw6sm13th。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:47:- 判：LM-ReID single > ablation single → consistency 训练有独立价值（强 method）；≈ → 只是"LR 增强 + 测试时 lattice ensemble"（弱，test-time trick 级）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:53:| h | single | lattice-mean | lattice-MaxSim | TTA-MaxSim | **LATTICE−TTA** |
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:59:- epoch 40 > epoch 10（h=16 single 69.96→75.71，+5.7），lattice-marg 增益随 h 递减（+2.33→+1.01→+0.71）= 符合"分辨率升高 lattice uncertainty 消退"机制，**三个 h 全 beat TTA**。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:60:- **诚实**：大头是 LR fine-tune（single 75.71 vs frozen single 42.65=+33，标准 CR-ReID，非创新）；**lattice-marg 真贡献 +2.33（robust，frozen +3.04/trained +3.006 一致 beat TTA）= 方法的 lattice-specific 贡献，但是 test-time-trick 量级**。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:61:- **method-vs-trick 终判仍靠 ablation（no-LM-loss）**：若 LM-ReID single > ablation single 则 consistency 训练有独立价值。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:65:h=16 single / lattice(best) / LATTICE−TTA：
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:67:| 配置 | single | lattice | LATTICE−TTA |
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:70:| **M=3 no-LM-loss**（增强但关 L_marg/L_cons） | **77.44** | **79.90** | **+2.576**（SANITY 88.92） |
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:71:| **M=3 full LM-ReID** | 75.71 | 78.04 | +3.006 |
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:73:- **full − M=1 = +2.09**（single+lattice 都 +2.09）= M=3 增强 + consistency 合计。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:74:- **★★★命门结果（2026-06-25）= 证伪 method**：no-LM-loss single **77.44 > full 75.71（+1.73）**！**consistency 训练（L_marg+L_cons）= HARMFUL 有害**，不是无用。分解：M=3 增强 +3.82（73.62→77.44，大）；consistency −1.73（77.44→75.71，过度正则压判别性）。SANITY 也证 no-LM HR 88.92 > full 86.09。**LM-ReID 作 method 死**（以 consistency 为创新，比简单 M=3 增强+测试时边缘化更差）。剩 test-time lattice-marg robust（五配置 beat TTA）但 trick（codex 5/10）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:75:- lattice-marg 三配置都 robust beat TTA（M=1 +3.727 > full +3.006：越不 lattice-invariant 的模型、测试时边缘化获益越多 = "训练内化测试时边缘化"干净机制故事）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:78:## ★salvage（2026-06-25 codex 后）：LM-ReID 没全死，是 consistency 版死了
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:80:salvage codex（`litreview2/lmreid_salvage.md`）：主救法 = **重构**，不卖 consistency，卖"**lattice-aware augmentation 增判别性 + test-time 对 lattice hidden variable 边缘化**（非压成同一 embedding）"。信心 6→7/10 after 命门对照，CCF-B 有条件可行。核心：no-LM-loss 77.44 已最强 = "invariance 死、lattice hidden variable + marginalization 没死"。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:82:**★命门对照（4090 跑中 `b46h62df9`）= M=3 ordinary-aug no-LM**（同 3 views/h/步数，variants 换普通 random crop/flip/color，非 lattice）：
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:83:- ≈77+（=lattice no-LM）→ M=3 增益只是"更多增广"，training-side 创新缩水 → 纯 test-time。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:84:- 明显低（−1.0+）→ **lattice-aug 是 lattice-specific** → 改名 **Lattice-Aware Augmentation + Lattice-Marginalized Retrieval** 重投。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:87:**★命门结果（2026-06-26）= lattice-aug 训练端不特殊（仅 +0.54）**：
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:89:| h=16 | single | lattice | LATTICE−TTA | SANITY |
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:92:| no-LM-loss（M=3 lattice） | **77.44** | 79.90 | +2.576 | 88.92 |
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:94:- lattice-aug 仅 **+0.54** over ordinary-aug（阈值 −1.0+ 才算 lattice-specific）；高 h 更平（h=24 84.00≈84.28，h=32 86.48≈86.44）→ **M=3 训练端增益主要是"更多增广"，lattice 训练不特殊**，salvage 主路 (a) 弱。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:95:- **★不对称（干净）**：test-time lattice-marg 四配置全 beat TTA（M=1 +3.727 / ordinary-aug +1.624 / lattice no-LM +2.576 / full +3.006）→ **lattice 在 test-time 是真 hidden variable，在 training 不特殊**。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:96:- 剩：(b) 纯 test-time（robust 但 codex 4.5/10）/ (c) Hard-Lattice ERM（被命门削弱）/ (d) pivot。**post-命门 codex（53466，bulrvjdg2）定夺**：含"test-time 特殊 / training 不特殊"不对称能否成更干净卖点。cons-only（3090）仍跑（诊断 L_cons vs L_marg，现次要）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:100:no-LM-loss ckpt，h=12/16/20/24/32，single vs lattice-marg vs 普通TTA（MaxSim）：
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:102:| h | single | lattice | LATTICE−TTA | 普通TTA gain |
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:110:- ① lattice-marg **全 5 分辨率 beat 普通TTA**（robust）；② **优势随 h 单调递减**（+6.5→+0.5）= sampling-lattice 是 severe-LR nuisance 的干净证据；③ **severe LR（h=12/16）普通 TTA 反而有害**，lattice-marg 大涨 = clean contrast（防"普通 TTA"质疑）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:111:- 强化 test-time decision-marginalization 故事。下一步 LM-S3（logsumexp 聚合）/ LM-S4（phase/bbox/kernel 因子）+ 更强 TTA baseline（kill-switch make_tta_variants 加 color/resize）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:112:- **cons-only eval 结果（L_cons 诊断，2026-06-26）**：single **76.895**（h=16）vs no-LM-loss 77.44 / full 75.71 → **L_cons 单独只 −0.55，L_marg 才是大元凶（≈−1.18）**（codex 预测"L_cons 主杀"错）。**强化 asymmetry：训练时边缘化 L_marg 有害 / 测试时边缘化有益**——decision-level marginalization 故事更锐。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:113:- **LM-S4 因子分解（h=16，single 77.44）**：phase +1.758 / **bbox +2.842（最大）** / zoom +1.702 / all +2.557 → **bbox 轴（±1 LR-pixel 检测框偏移）主导**，9 bbox > 3+3+3 混合。codex 预测 phase 不对，但 bbox 故事更直观（severe LR 检测框 ±1 LR 像素 = 几个 HR 像素 = 真实 crop 不确定性）。三轴全 beat TTA。→ nuisance 主要是 **LR 检测框/crop 不确定性**。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:114:- LM-S2-strong（强 TTA 防御）还在跑；**LM-S5 Hard-Lattice ERM**（3090，bqd5skbum，loss_mode=hard 已实现+smoke 过）epoch 0，待 Hard-ordinary 对照（需 Hard-Lat ≥+0.8 over Hard-ord 才算训练端活）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:116:novelty：无 exact prior（sampling-lattice as LR-ReID hidden variable + marginalization）；BlurPool(aliasing)/FlipReID(flip consistency) 是相关先例；"consistency 有害"不单独成 paper 但强消融卖点。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:117:training-side 转向备选（若 ordinary-aug 抹平 lattice 特异性）：Hard-Lattice ERM/CVaR（优化最难 variant 的 CE+triplet，非压 embedding）/ set-wise retrieval training。

experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:1:# LM-ReID 训练端创新 pipeline（2026-06-26，用户指令"训练端不许停"后）
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:3:## ★更新（2026-06-26 凌晨）：frozen-feature 类全死 → 转 LSRC（改 backbone）
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:5:- **LS-MRT 死**：冻 backbone probe，smoke 时 P(D×D linear ~1M params)过拟合暴跌 −8.694；修复（identity-reg + 降 lr + 全量 116k samples）后 **+0.028 clean FAIL**（K-cosine 不升，但 P 不帮忙）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:6:- **LPA 死**：query-side 加权 +0.075，预测最佳变体 acc 12.4%≈chance。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:7:- **关键发现**：LS-MRT(+0.028)+LPA(+0.075) 两个冻结特征 probe 都 ~0 → **no-LM-loss 特征对 test-time 边缘化已近最优，重投影/重加权救不了**（oracle +4.338 是 gallery 真 ID 上界，frozen-feature 够不着）。**LCRS/LRFD/DeepSets 也是 frozen-feature 重投影，大概率同样死**。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:12:| **LSRC** | 7.5/10 | full fine-tune，`L_id + lam_lsrc*(bag-to-bag set-supcon logsumexp + **neg-tail 压负样本假高 lattice**)`。打 marginalization 数学瓶颈（更少假高负例+更多可复用正证据），**解释了 frozen 为何没空间**（lattice union 已固定） | 不依赖原图 ✓ | **进行中** 4090 lam0.5 + 3090 lam1.0，acc 0.98/0.97 健康，判据 lattice mAP > 79.90 +0.3 且 single 不掉 |
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:13:| BLC/LC-STN++ | 8/10→6.5 | bbox crop refiner 改输入（最贴 bbox 主导发现） | market 只裁好框→6.5 | 备选 |
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:16:## （旧）5 候选 frozen-feature pipeline（LS-MRT 已死，其余大概率同死）
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:18:LPA(A 加权头)定死（oracle headroom +4.338 不可达，最佳变体 query+gallery 共定单看 query 预测不出 acc≈chance）。Hard-Lattice ERM eval 中。按用户"这三个不行就找更多"启 4 新 codex（litreview2/train_more_*.md），收敛到 **5 个候选**，全避开已死的（invariance-collapse / query-side 预测最佳变体 / L_marg 分类头边缘化）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:22:| **LS-MRT**（set-wise 检索） | 7/10 | 把 test-time 边缘化写进**训练检索损失**：`S=logmeanexp_k sim(z_q,k, z_g)`，supervised contrastive over gallery。**在检索决策层边缘化非分类头**——直接修 L_marg 失败因（L_marg 在 train-ID classifier posterior 求均值→塌缩；LS-MRT 在 q-g 相似度证据边缘化，denominator 有真负 gallery） | 冻 backbone + cached K=9 features 训小 P(linear/BNNeck+τ)，**最廉价**；活=h12/16 ≥+0.3 且 K-cosine 不升 | **先跑** |
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:23:| LCRS（互补 residual） | 7/10 | `z_k = norm(P_shared(g_k) + α·P_k(g_k))`，shared identity core + lattice-specific residual subspace + decorr(只在分类正确后)。"准确后分工"非硬推开。test K=9 边缘化拿更富 union | 冻 backbone 训 P_shared/P_k/cls；活=K-error correlation 降+K=9 gain ≥+0.5，individual variant mAP 不掉>0.8 | 排队 |
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:24:| LRFD（disentangle） | 7/10 | `z_l=P_id, r_l=P_lat`(lattice nuisance sink, 必须能预测 lattice), per-variant CE+triplet, 推理丢 r_l 用 z_l 边缘化 | 冻 backbone 训 heads；活=with-lattice-code >+0.2 over without + r_l lattice-pred acc>60% | 排队 |
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:25:| LC-STN（对齐 canonicalize） | 7/10 | tiny localization net 预测 bbox offset(dx,dy)+grid_sample 重采样到 canonical, L_geo=SmoothL1 监督几何非身份, 残差留 marginalization | 冻 backbone 训 canonicalizer；活=offset MAE<0.35 LR px + K=1 +0.8 OR K=9 +0.3 | 排队 |
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:26:| DeepSets Marginalizer（LPA 修正） | 6.5/10 | 不预测 query 最佳变体(LPA 死因)，改 pairwise: 每个(q,g)的 K 个 cos 经 DeepSets φ/ρ 合成校准分, α 初 0 限幅, embedding 冻 | cached features 小 scorer；活=+0.4 over uniform, 且 ordinary-TTA scorer 追不平(否则只是 generic learned TTA) | 排队 |
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:28:**不做**（codex 共判）：TTT/TENT(无标签自我确认 3/10), DRO/Hard-Lattice(≈将死 4/10), PFE-Gaussian(query-side uncertainty 不够 4/10)。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:30:**执行顺序**：LS-MRT 冻 probe 先（最廉价+最强故事）→ 活则全量；死则 LCRS→LRFD→LC-STN→DeepSets 逐个 cheap probe。**任何一个过线即训练端第二 contribution，凑成 train+test 完整方法（→7-8/10）。全死也不停——再启 codex 找更多。** novelty 共识：卖点不是各机制本身(decorr/disentangle/STN/DeepSets 都老)，是"**LR 采样格点当可枚举隐变量 + 训练端 X + 测试端 decision marginalization**"这个 problem reformulation + train/test 对齐。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview/reviews/lit_8.md:619:- A 语义/VLM teacher: 已完成,见 experiments/paper_materials/newinfo_semantic_teacher-codex.md。结论是遮挡语义 teacher 对 SOLIDER 强主干整体偏负,FLaN-Net/π-VL/AG-ReID/LVLM-ReID/RMPSNet 等发表遮挡成绩多低于或接近现有强基线; 唯一值得先探的是属性 teacher 的可靠性噪声屏蔽,其次是 teacher 只用于属性级样本组织,两者都必须先做零训练探针。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/claude_review.md:1:# Claude Broad Review — exp359 LM-ReID 训练脚本
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/claude_review.md:8:- **不是小调参**：LM-ReID 是新机制（lattice-marginalized embedding），重定义低分辨率 ReID = 采样格点不确定性；有零训练 kill-switch GO（独立轴、双 trivial 控制 vs-TTA/vs-#false 都过、GO 靠 interventional 直接测量）做前置，codex 判 CCF-B 7/10。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/claude_review.md:9:- **单变量**：唯一变量 = "训练 lattice-invariance" vs "frozen 模型"。eval 复用 kill-switch（byte-identical degradation），与 frozen baseline 只差"是否 fine-tune"。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/claude_review.md:23:- **L_adv** = GRL：disc 预测 variant slot，梯度反转使 z 不含可预测 lattice label。`slot=arange(M).repeat(B)` 与 image-major reshape 对齐。warmup-gated（adv_start），默认 lam_adv=0（弱辅助先不开，可后续 ablation）。✓
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/claude_review.md:29:- lattice 生成函数（make_lr/make_lattice_variants/pil_to_tensor_np/_to_target_aspect）**逐行复制自 cvpb_lattice_killswitch.py** → 训练降质与 GO kill-switch eval **byte-identical**（单变量隔离的关键）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/claude_review.md:43:逐行审查 + 源码接口验证完成，4 处问题已修（KL 方向 / AMP / 稳健 out 取值 / scaler backward）。代码正确、对照隔离干净（lattice 生成 byte-identical 复用、eval 复用 kill-switch）、过线判据明确、诚实判死路径清楚。**审查通过**，可进 smoke + 训练（仍需 Codex 独立审通过）。

experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:30:# 实验 exp359: LM-ReID (Lattice-Marginalized ReID)
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:32:> 后 PRCV「换问题」阶段第一个 GO 的 method 候选（9 个零训练 cheap-kill 全死后）。零训练 kill-switch 已 GO（d8 lattice, agent a68e6），method-design codex 判 **CCF-B 7/10**（训练版超 frozen ensemble → 8/10）。本实验做训练版，证明 lattice-marginalization 是 **method 不是 ensemble trick**。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:33:> 设计来源：`experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt`（完整 codex 设计）+ `cvpb_lattice_result.md`（kill-switch 数据）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:37:低分辨率 ReID 的传统视角：LR = 模糊/缺细节，解法 = SR / resolution-invariant feature。**我们重定义**：一部分 LR 失败不是"缺信息"，而是 **采样格点不确定性（sampling-lattice uncertainty）**——同一个 HR 身份在不同合法的 LR 采样格点（sub-pixel phase / bbox alignment / downsample kernel）下，落到不同 embedding 区域，导致 rank-1 身份翻转。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:40:frozen exp260b Market，K=9 lattice variants ensemble，HR gallery / LR query：
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:49:两个生死控制都过：**vs 普通 TTA**（同 K 同 MaxSim，lattice 比 TTA 多 +3.04，证 lattice-specific 非 TTA 换名；lat-MaxSim 46.9 > tta 43.8 > single 42.6，无"输给 trivial baseline"问题）；**vs #false-in-topk**（phase-var partial +0.19/+0.21 存活）。机制：phase-var 是 TTA-var 的 **7.5×**；74.9% h=16 query 跨 lattice 翻转 rank-1，随 h 升单调衰减到 1.2%（h=48 机制消失 = 低分辨率专用现象）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:52:phase-var 作 per-query 失败预测器**不干净**（控 LR-severity 后 partial 塌到 ≈0，与 per-image LR 失真共线）。**GO 靠的是 interventional 结果**（ensemble +4.2 / LAT−TTA +3.0 是直接测量）。故事写成 "lattice sensitivity 是 **mechanism-level nuisance**, 不是 standalone failure predictor"，方法是对所有 severe-LR query 做 marginalization（不是预测哪张失败）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:56:训练一个 lattice-marginalized embedding（对 lattice variants 身份稳定）+ 推理 K-marginalization，在 h=16 上比 frozen lattice ensemble **再高 +0.8~2.0 mAP** → 证明它学到了 lattice-invariance（是 method），不是 ensemble trick。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:62:2. fine-tune：HR train image 在线生成 LR lattice variants `x^l = U(D_l(x))`，l ∈ {sub-pixel phase, bbox jitter, downsample kernel}。每图每 iter 采样 M=2-4 variants，eval 用 K=9。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:65:### LM-ReID loss
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:69:L_marg = -log[ mean_l p^l[y] ] + Triplet(z^μ, y)                 # marginal likelihood（主贡献）
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:71:L_adv  = GRL-CE(Dφ(z^l), lattice_label_l)                        # 弱：去掉 embedding 中可预测 lattice label
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:76:### 推理 K-marginalization
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:87:- h=32：允许 marginal 不负。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:89:失败最可能原因：训练版只 ≈ frozen ensemble（没学到额外 lattice-invariance）→ 沦为 test-time ensemble trick，不成方法稿。备选投稿角度：同等 mAP 下 K 从 9 降到 3 或 single inference 保留大部分收益。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:95:- **frozen lattice ensemble**（零训练 K=9，= kill-switch 的 +4.23，这是训练版必须超过的硬线）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:98:消融：marg only / marg+cons / marg+cons+adv；τ sweep；K=1/3/5/9 曲线；phase-only vs +bbox+kernel。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:102:- 合成：Market/MSMT，gallery HR，query LR h=16/24/32，canonical LR single baseline，K=9（3×3 phase 主，bbox/kernel ablation，不无限扩 K）。所有 TTA 对照 **K-matched**。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:104:- 新指标：PRF@1（phase rank-flip rate）、Flip Entropy、LEG（lattice ensemble gain）、LOTG（lattice-over-TTA gain）、query ΔAP。按 h 分报 + paired bootstrap 95% CI + K=1/3/5/9 曲线 + compute cost。PRF 随 h（74.9%→31%→10%→1.2%）是强故事线。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:108:- **VPFA**（2510.00936，最近邻）：CR-ReID = feature-space resolution direction，Vector Panning LR→pseudo-HR。hidden variable 是 resolution gap/feature direction，**不是 sampling lattice，不做 lattice marginalization**。必须正面对比。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:109:- LRAR（2207.13037）：resolution-adaptive representation，占"resolution adaptive"词 → 我们 novelty 写 sampling-lattice uncertainty。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:110:- RFD（2109.07871）：multi-res gallery distillation，占"resolution-invariant distillation"，没占 lattice intervention。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:111:- BlurPool（1904.11486）：anti-aliasing/shift-invariance 已老 → **不能说首次发现 aliasing 影响**。**能声称**：首次在 LR person ReID 把采样格点作隐藏变量 + rank-level intervention 证明 + lattice-specific marginalization 解决检索身份翻转。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:112:- FlipReID（2105.05639）：ReID 常用 flip-mean TTA → **必须反复强调非 TTA 换名**（同 K vs-TTA 控制，lattice 多 +3.04）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:116:operating point 低（只在 h≤24 强 +3-4 mAP，h=32 marginal）→ 主动收窄定位 **"severe low-resolution / cross-resolution ReID under sampling-lattice uncertainty"**，不写成通用 ReID 鲁棒性稿。h=32 marginal 反而支持机制（分辨率升高 → lattice uncertainty 消退）。真正风险 = 训练版打不过 frozen ensemble，那就只是 ensemble trick。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:121:2. 实现 LM-ReID（插件式，config 开关，lattice aug 复用 `cvpb_lattice_killswitch.py` 的 LR 生成）。

experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:2996:  Fix: make real training default `M>=4` at minimum, or sample perturbation mode/kernel for each noncanonical variant; if keeping `L_adv`, store actual lattice labels.
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:3001:- **Medium** [cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:345](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:345): `L_id` triplet is computed over flattened `B*M`, not `mean_l Triplet(gf[:, l], y)`. This treats other variants of the same image as positives and is not exactly the stated per-lattice-slot formula.

experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:3018:I did not find an exact prior for “sampling-lattice hidden variable / sub-pixel phase marginalization” in low-res ReID. Closest works model resolution gap or resolution-invariant features, not lattice phase marginalization: VPFA, LRAR, RFD; BlurPool covers general anti-aliasing/shift-invariance but not ReID lattice marginalization. Sources: [VPFA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00936), [LRAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2207.13037), [RFD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2109.07871), [BlurPool](https://arxiv.org/abs/1904.11486).

This contradicts design “每图每 iter 采样 M variants”.

experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:1:# 实验 exp359-LSRC: Lattice-Set Retrieval Contrastive（训练端机制）
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:3:> ⚠️ **流程补记**：本 design 是**事后补写**（2026-06-26）。LSRC 已在 smoke 通过后直接启动全量训练，违反了"训练前先写 design + 双审查"铁律。现补 design + codex 独立审查（codex_lsrc_review.md），若审出 Critical 立即 kill 重跑。教训记入 memory `pre-experiment-review-discipline`。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:7:- test-time decision-marginalization（对 K=9 lattice 变体边缘化检索决策）已成立（6/10），但纯 test-time。用户要训练端互补机制凑成完整方法。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:8:- **frozen-feature 类训练端机制全部证负**：LS-MRT（set-retrieval 重投影 linear P）+0.028、LPA（query-side 加权）+0.075，两个冻结特征 probe 都 ≈0。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:9:- **诊断**：no-LM-loss 的冻结特征对 test-time 边缘化已近最优——lattice union 固定，重投影/重加权没有 headroom（oracle +4.338 是 gallery 真 ID 上界，frozen 够不着）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:10:- → 训练端价值必须**改特征本身（backbone）**。codex（train2_backbone）收敛到 LSRC，7.5/10。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:14:一句话：**训 backbone 让 K 个 lattice 变体形成"可边缘化的证据集"**——让负样本不产生假高 lattice 相似（marginalization 的数学瓶颈），让正样本有多路可匹配证据——比 frozen 重投影有空间，因为 backbone 能改变 aliasing/bbox-shift 敏感的特征本身。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:19:- **数据流**：B 张图各生成 M 个 lattice 变体 → backbone → `gf_bm [B,M,D]` → L2 norm → bag-to-bag `sim4 = einsum('ikd,jld->ijkl')` [B,B,M×M] cos → `S = tau_l·logsumexp(sim4/tau_l)` [B,B] set score。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:22:  - `L_negtail`：`softplus((max_neg_lattice_pair_cos - margin)/0.1)`，压低负样本对的最大 lattice-pair 相似度（核心：marg 最怕某错 lattice 假高分）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:23:  - `L_cov`（codex step2，默认关）：top-M 正样本 lattice-pair，正样本不靠单一固定 lattice 赢。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:24:- **关键超参**：lam_lsrc 0.5(4090)/1.0(3090) hedge，lsrc_tau 0.1，lsrc_margin 0.4，lr 1e-3，40 epoch，`--lam_marg 0 --lam_cons 0`（隔离 LSRC）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:28:- 假设成立：lattice mAP > no-LM-loss 79.90 **+0.3~0.5**，且 single_mean 不掉 >0.8（单变体判别力保住）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:29:- 失败最可能原因：(a) set-supcon 退化成普通 instance 判别，和 L_id 冗余（→ +0）；(b) neg-tail 压过头连正样本 lattice 也压（→ single 掉）；(c) 又一次"backbone 已最优、训练端无空间"。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:34:- **baseline**：no-LM-loss（exp359_abl_noLMloss）= 训练端不动、只 test-time 边缘化 → lattice 79.90 / single ~76.9。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:35:- **消融变量**：只开 LSRC（lam_marg=0, lam_cons=0），单变量隔离 LSRC 贡献。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:42:- 非 **Hard-Lattice ERM**（76.894 死）：把 K 变体当检索证据集，非独立硬样本逐个 ERM。

experiments/exp096/design.md:11:- **Stage 3 (12×4, 768-dim)**：高语义、低分辨率 → 捕获身份级特征
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_final.txt:8:- **High** [cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:173](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:173): per-sample RNG is reset from `seed + idx`, so each image gets the same height and same lattice variants every epoch. This contradicts design “每图每 iter 采样 M variants”.
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_final.txt:11:- **High** [cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:48](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:48), [cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:118](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:118): default `M=2` plus `mode = j % 3` trains only canonical + bbox-shift/bilinear. It does not train sub-pixel phase or zoom/antialias axes despite the method claim.
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_final.txt:12:  Fix: make real training default `M>=4` at minimum, or sample perturbation mode/kernel for each noncanonical variant; if keeping `L_adv`, store actual lattice labels.
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_final.txt:17:- **Medium** [cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:345](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:345): `L_id` triplet is computed over flattened `B*M`, not `mean_l Triplet(gf[:, l], y)`. This treats other variants of the same image as positives and is not exactly the stated per-lattice-slot formula.
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_final.txt:20:- **Low** [cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:96](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:96): lattice generation appears output-equivalent to kill-switch, but not literally byte-identical source.  
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_final.txt:34:I did not find an exact prior for “sampling-lattice hidden variable / sub-pixel phase marginalization” in low-res ReID. Closest works model resolution gap or resolution-invariant features, not lattice phase marginalization: VPFA, LRAR, RFD; BlurPool covers general anti-aliasing/shift-invariance but not ReID lattice marginalization. Sources: [VPFA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00936), [LRAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2207.13037), [RFD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2109.07871), [BlurPool](https://arxiv.org/abs/1904.11486).

experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_options.md:1:# LM-ReID 训练端创新候选（2026-06-26，3-codex 头脑风暴综合）
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_options.md:6:训练端做 invariance / feature-collapse 压判别性 = 死路：consistency −1.73、lattice-aug 不特殊 +0.54、L_marg 训练有害大元凶。新机制必须绕开"逼 K 变体特征塌成一个点"。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_options.md:8:## 机制 A：学怎么加权边缘化（LPA / LUD）— codex 最高 7.5/10
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_options.md:9:- **LPA（Lattice Posterior Amortization，lens3 #1）**：冻结 backbone，只训小头预测 posterior qφ(z|x)（z=bbox/phase/kernel 格点），测试时 `score = logsumexp_z[log qφ(z|q) + cos(fθ(Tz q), fθ(g))/τ]`，uniform → posterior 加权。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_options.md:10:- **LUD（Lattice Uncertainty Distillation，lens2）= 同族**：训头预测 lattice 风险 risk_k（stopgrad teacher 的 spread + margin），测试 per-variant 加权。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_options.md:13:- **kill-switch**：冻 no-LM-loss ckpt + 训 qφ 1-3 epoch；weighted 比 uniform 多 ≥+0.4 mAP(h12/16) + 格点预测 acc ≥35% + LATTICE−TTA 不降 → 活，否则杀。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_options.md:14:- **不撞 PFE**：PFE=generic data-quality uncertainty；LPA=supervised sampling-lattice posterior（隐变量来源明确 = LR 采样格点，监督来自 K-hypothesis empirical spread）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_options.md:16:## 机制 B：学怎么对齐检测框（LC-STN / BLC）— 7/10（lens1）/ 6.5/10（lens3）
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_options.md:17:- 训小模块**估计 LR crop 格点偏移 (dx,dy) 并 grid_sample 重采样到 canonical**（类 STN，但监督几何参数非身份特征），残差留 test-time marginalization。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_options.md:18:- 第一版 translation-only sub-pixel re-centering（max_shift=1.25 LR px，tanh bound），只打 bbox 主因子。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_options.md:22:- **novelty**：STN/AlignedReID/PAN/STNReID/CDPM 先例多 → 卖点不是"用 STN"，是"LR detector crop lattice 当可边缘化隐变量 + 监督 canonicalization 非 invariance + 残差 decision marginalization"组合无先例。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_options.md:25:- raw Hard-Lattice ERM/CVaR（太像更狠 lattice-aug，续压判别性）→ 只做 cheap ablation（= 正在跑的 LM-S5，验证后即停）
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_options.md:29:- 两个 kill-switch 都冻结 backbone、廉价 → **两个都验，先 A（置信高）后 B（分量大）**。过的那个 = 训练端第二 contribution，和 test-time marginalization 组成完整 train+test 方法（推 7-8/10）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_options.md:31:- 当前 GPU 被 Hard-Lattice(3090)/Hard-ordinary(4090) 占 → 先实现两个 kill-switch 代码，GPU 一空即 cheap probe（不直接堆训练）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:8:低分辨率 ReID 里，LR 观测只是 HR 场景在一个**未知采样格点**（sub-pixel phase / ±1 LR-pixel 检测框 crop / antialias kernel）下的一次采样；正确处理不是训练端逼特征不变，而是**检索时对格点隐变量做决策级边缘化**。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:13:- **因子分解（LM-S4）**: bbox 检测框 crop 不确定性主导（+2.84）> sub-pixel phase（+1.76）> zoom（+1.70）。→ mechanism = LR detector crop lattice，直观且新。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:16:1. **问题重定义** + 诊断: sampling lattice 是 LR-ReID 此前未显式建模的 test-time hidden nuisance；rank-level 干预证明它造成身份翻转。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:17:2. **方法（test-time）**: 决策级 lattice marginalization（K 变体 + mean/max/logsumexp 聚合），并证明 **decision-level marginalization > training-level invariance**。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:18:3. **方法（train-time，待定）**: A=LPA posterior 加权 / B=LC-STN canonicalization（廉价 kill-switch 验证中，过的进正文，否则进 discussion）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:21:- **LM-S2 inference 主实验**: lattice-marg 在 h=12/16/20/24/32 **全 beat 普通 TTA**，优势随分辨率单调递减（h12 +6.5 → h32 +0.5）= sampling-lattice 是 severe-LR nuisance 的干净证据；severe LR（h12/16）普通 TTA 反而有害。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:22:- **LM-S2-strong 防御**: 用更强 TTA（pad-crop + resize-jitter + color）当 baseline，lattice 仍赢且 gap 更大（h16 LATTICE−TTA +3.337）→ 堵死"不就是多裁几次"质疑。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:23:- **LM-S4 因子消融**: bbox 轴主导（见上）。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:24:- **训练端反例（强消融卖点）**: consistency 拉特征到均值有害（−1.73 还掉 HR sanity）；lattice-aug 训练 ≈ 普通 random 增广（只 +0.54 不 lattice-specific，命门对照）；marginal-likelihood L_marg 是训练有害大元凶（cons-only 诊断）。→ 坐实"训练端 invariance/collapse 压判别性"，反衬 test-time marginalization。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:27:- **train-time A/B**: LPA oracle headroom（cheap kill-switch）→ LPA 学习头 OR LC-STN canonicalizer。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:28:- **Hard-Lattice ERM**（LM-S5，跑中）: 训练端 worst-case，codex 预测大概率负 → 进 discussion "为何 robust-ERM 也救不了训练端"。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:35:- TTA（k-reciprocal CVPR17 进 SOTA / FlipReID / BNTA）: test-time 后处理可发先例；我们是 lattice-specific marginalization 非通用 TTA。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:36:- Aliasing（BlurPool）: shift-variance 已知，但我们不是"发现 aliasing"，是"把 sampling lattice 当可边缘化隐变量"。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:37:- Alignment（STN/AlignedReID/STNReID/PAN/CDPM）: 若用 B LC-STN，卖点是"LR crop lattice 当隐变量 + 监督 canonicalization 非 invariance + 残差 marginalization"组合无先例。
experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:38:- Uncertainty（PFE/DUL/UMTS）: 若用 A LPA，卖点是 supervised sampling-lattice posterior 非 generic data-quality。
experiments/paper_notes/paper_17_DPEFormer.md:67:2. 训练时随机选取一个 mask，缩放到占行人 bbox 面积的 50-75%

experiments/exp324/design.md:11:- **我们的咬合**：MaxSim 天然吃 DINO dense tokens（late-interaction，非全局池化）；pose+5-part 把 DINO 在低分辨率人物上 noisy 的 correspondence 约束到 body-part 语义上降噪；只比 mutually-visible part 对症遮挡。24G 最稳、不依赖大模型、最复用现成 MaxSim 全套。
experiments/exp324/design.md:28:- 失败最可能原因：(1) DINO correspondence 在 128×256 低分辨率脏 crop 上漂移严重，pose 降噪不够；(2) part-MaxSim 不带训练直接 eval 太弱（DINO 特征非 ReID-judiciable）；(3) 只在整体涨、重遮挡组不涨。任一即降级。

experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lattice_killswitch_DESIGN.md:3:> 脚本: `cvpb_lattice_killswitch.py`  机会来源: `litreview2/explore20/clean/d_8.txt` 机会1（信心 7/10）
experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lattice_killswitch_DESIGN.md:7:低分辨率 ReID 失败**不是**"模糊缺细节"，而是**采样格点不确定性**：一个人在 h=16-32px 时 = 一族 alias/crop-lattice 观测（亚像素采样相位 / ±1 LR-pixel bbox 量化 / antialias kernel / 轻微 detector crop error）。模型只见过其中一个。身份匹配应对这族观测**边缘化**，而不是把某个确定性 LR 图当真值。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lattice_killswitch_DESIGN.md:13:- **K=9 lattice 变体**（每 LR query，从同一 HR 图）：variant0 = 确定性 bicubic LR（= 单图 baseline）；其余轮转覆盖三轴：亚像素相位平移（±0.5 LR 像素的 HR 仿射）、±1 LR-pixel bbox crop shift、bbox expand/contract 1 LR 像素 + kernel 切换（bicubic/bilinear/lanczos/box/hamming）。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lattice_killswitch_DESIGN.md:17:1. **vs 普通 TTA**：相同 K、相同融合（mean / MaxSim），但 K 个 view 是普通 test-time aug（pad+RandomCrop + hflip）of 单一 bicubic LR，**无 lattice 语义**。phase-lattice 必须明显超过普通 TTA（否则只是 TTA 换名）。MaxSim 对照尤其关键：MaxSim 取 K 中最优可能仅靠"多枪"涨点，TTA-MaxSim 同样多枪 → 差值才是 lattice 特有增益。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lattice_killswitch_DESIGN.md:27:- K 选 9：覆盖三轴 + variant0 = 单图 baseline（lattice 与 TTA 共享 variant0，保证 baseline 一致）。

experiments/cargo_cvpb/new_angle_AIRL.md:8:- 用 bbox 像素高/面积、camera id、估计 altitude/view 生成 **degradation ladder**, 把 ground 图降解到不同 UAV 像素预算。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/new_angle_AIRL.md:18:1. **零训练诊断(先做)**: 用现有 Swin baseline 按 aerial bbox height/area 分桶, 算 per-bucket A→G mAP。**判据: 低尺度桶相对高尺度桶塌陷(gap > 3-5 mAP)→ resolution 是主误差源, 角度成立; 不塌 → 杀(尺度不是主因)。**
experiments/cargo_cvpb/new_angle_AIRL.md:19:2. **最小退化实验**: ground 图按 aerial bbox 尺度分布下采样/模糊/JPEG + prediction consistency 短训。**判据: Swin 上 A→G 涨 ≥1.0 或最低像素桶涨 ≥3.0 且 reliability AUROC ≥0.65 → 继续; 否则杀。**
experiments/cargo_cvpb/new_angle_AIRL.md:31:脚本 `airl_scale_diag.py`(lab-3090)。按 aerial query 原生 bbox 像素分 4 等量分位桶, 各桶对同一全量 ground gallery 算 A->G mAP(gallery 跨桶不变→桶差只归因 aerial 尺度)。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/new_angle_AIRL.md:46:**下一步(按 design 的 kill-switch 阶梯 #2)**: 最小退化实验 —— ground 图按 aerial bbox 尺度分布下采样/模糊/JPEG + prediction-consistency 短训。判据: Swin 上 A->G 涨 ≥1.0 或最低像素桶涨 ≥3.0 且 reliability AUROC ≥0.65 → 继续建机制; 否则杀。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/new_angle_AIRL.md:52:AIRL-Swin 训练完成(lab-4090, `cvpb_airl_swin2`, ep60, `--airl --airl_lambda 0.5 --airl_min_scale 0.25 --consistency kl --warmup 5`, 无新可学参数, eval 路径与 baseline 逐键相同/379 键 0 多余)。对照 baseline-Swin(`cvpb_swin_baseline256`, ep60)。两个 model_best 各按 aerial query 原生 bbox 尺度分等量分位桶, 对**同一全量 ground gallery** 算 per-bucket A->G mAP。桶边界由图像文件尺度决定(模型无关)→ 同一桶跨两 ckpt 是**同一批 query**, Δ 干净归因于 AIRL。两 baseline FULL A->G==训练 log ep60(58.75)→ pipeline 精确。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/new_angle_AIRL.md:92:- area gate(单模型可近似): 按 query 原生 bbox area 路由(低 area→AIRL), 阈值 = CARGO **train** 分位(非 test 调)。

experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train_lens1_align.md:14:ReID团队 LM-ReID(低分辨率person ReID 重定义为采样格点sampling-lattice隐变量,非模糊): test-time decision-marginalization已成立(LR-query vs HR-gallery, 同图K=9个lattice变体[sub-pixel phase/±1 LR-pixel bbox/antialias kernel]做边缘化, Market h=12/16/20/24/32全beat普通TTA, 优势随分辨率单调递减h12 +6.5→h32 +0.5; 因子分解=**bbox检测框±1 LR-pixel的crop不确定性主导+2.84** > phase +1.76 > zoom +1.70)。**训练端已试全负**: ①consistency(拉K变体特征到均值)有害single -1.73还掉HR sanity; ②lattice-aug(训练时见lattice变体)≈普通random增广只差+0.54不lattice-specific; ③marginal-likelihood L_marg是训练有害大元凶。**铁律: 训练端做invariance/feature-collapse压判别性=死路**。目标=一个**互补的训练端创新**和test-time marginalization结合成train+test完整方法(更有把握, 6/10→7-8/10)。 ★lens=对齐/规范化而非不变: 既然nuisance主要是LR bbox-crop不确定性, 训练一个模块**学着把LR crop对齐/重定心**(类spatial transformer/学到的re-centering/sub-pixel配准)让特征canonicalize而非collapse(训练降lattice spread+测试边缘化残差)。求: 具体机制(网络结构/loss/怎么和marginalization接) + 为何不重蹈invariance-collapse + 廉价kill-switch(冻结/小实验先验) + novelty(联网查STN-ReID/AlignedReID/sub-pixel-registration/spatial-alignment先例) + 信心1-10 + 务实中文。

experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train_lens1_align.md:40:短结论：**可以做，优先级高**。但主贡献不能叫“STN for ReID”，因为 STN/对齐/ReID 先例很多；应命名成 **sampling-lattice canonicalization for LR-query ReID**。核心不是“学不变性”，而是**估计 LR crop 的隐式采样格点偏移，并把输入重采样到 canonical lattice，再保留 test-time marginalization 处理残差不确定性**。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train_lens1_align.md:43:输入 LR query 原始低分辨率 crop，不先简单 resize。加一个很小的 localization head：
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train_lens1_align.md:62:不要一上来做 full affine / TPS / deformable grid。你的因子分解已经说明 bbox ±1 LR-pixel crop uncertainty 是主导项，所以第一版只打这个主因子。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train_lens1_align.md:64:训练时用已知合成 lattice 参数监督几何校正：
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train_lens1_align.md:67:L_geo = SmoothL1((dx, dy), -(delta_bbox + delta_phase))
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train_lens1_align.md:72:关键点：**不要加 K variants feature-to-mean consistency，不要训练 L_marg**。lattice spread 只做 monitor 指标，不做 feature collapse loss。

experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train_lens1_align.md:82:先用 uniform marginalization。后面如果 alignment confidence 校准可靠，再试：

experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train2_input.md:15:ReID LM-ReID(低分辨率ReID=采样格点sampling-lattice隐变量): test-time lattice marginalization成立6/10(K=9变体[phase/±1 LR-pixel bbox/kernel]边缘化, 5分辨率全beat普通TTA, bbox检测框不确定性主导+2.84)。**关键新发现(实测): 所有冻backbone的frozen-feature训练端probe都失败**: LS-MRT重投影linear P +0.028; LPA query-side加权 +0.075; LPA-oracle headroom +4.338但=gallery真ID上界(query/frozen-feature够不着)。**结论: no-LM-loss冻结特征对test-time marginalization已近最优, 重投影/重加权救不了。训练端价值必须来自(a)改变特征本身=backbone训练 或(b)改变输入=如LC-STN canonicalization重采样crop**。已死: consistency塌缩/L_marg有害/lattice-aug不特殊/Hard-Lattice ERM死/LPA死/LS-MRT-frozen死。求训练端机制FUNDAMENTALLY改特征或输入(非frozen-feature tweak)让marginalization涨更多, 避开invariance-collapse, 廉价kill-switch。给具体机制+为何能改善(不是又一个frozen重投影)+kill-switch+联网查novelty+信心1-10, 务实中文。 ★lens=改输入(input-level): LC-STN重采样canonical crop是一种; 还有什么训练端input-level机制(learned下采样核/anti-alias/可微bbox refine/sampling-aware augmentation生成)改变进backbone的图, 让marginalization残差更小或更有信息? 哪个最贴bbox检测框主导这个发现? kill-switch?
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train2_input.md:17:我先按你的 lens 聚焦 input-level，不再把 frozen feature 上的线性/加权 probe 当候选。先查近年低分辨率 ReID、可学习重采样/anti-alias、STN/bbox refinement 相关原始论文与代码线，确认哪些机制已经被做过，哪些还能作为新实验切入点。

experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train2_input.md:43:最贴你这个“bbox 检测框不确定性主导 +2.84”的发现的训练端 input-level 机制，不是 learned kernel，也不是普通 lattice aug，而是：
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train2_input.md:45:**BLC / LC-STN++: bbox-lattice canonical crop refiner**
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train2_input.md:46:训练一个轻量可微 crop refiner，在图像进 Swin-Tiny 之前，把带 bbox/phase 扰动的 crop 重新采样成 canonical crop；backbone 必须参与训练。test-time 仍保留 K=9 marginalization，但每个 lattice 先经过 refiner，再做特征平均或距离平均。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train2_input.md:50:bbox 误差先决定“哪些前景/背景进入网络”。learned downsample kernel 只能在已给定 crop support 内优化采样，救不了人被裁掉、背景占比偏移、头脚边界偏移这类错误。你现在测到 bbox 不确定性主导，说明主病灶在 support/crop window，而不是单纯 resize kernel。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train2_input.md:56:随机生成 bbox jitter / phase / ±1 LR-pixel 扰动
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train2_input.md:62:禁止: embedding consistency / L_marg / 强制 K 个 lattice feature 相等
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train2_input.md:69:已有工作里，STN 本身很老，原始 STN 已经是可微空间变换模块；ReID 里也有 STNReID 用 STN 做 partial ReID 对齐；person search 里也有 ReID-driven localization refinement，用 ReID loss 监督 bbox refinement。也就是说“STN/refine bbox”本身不能当新意。可争的新意是：把 **低分辨率 ReID 形式化为 sampling-lattice/bbox latent uncertainty**，训练 input canonicalizer，并和 test-time lattice marginalization 配套验证。参考：STN 原文、STNReID、ReID-driven localization refinement。([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/1506.02025)) ([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/1903.07072)) ([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/1909.08580))
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train2_input.md:75:| **1. BLC / LC-STN++ bbox canonicalizer** | bbox/crop support | 直接修正 bbox latent，降低错误背景/裁切造成的 lattice 方差，同时保留 residual marginalization | 单独不新；和 LM-ReID 的 lattice latent 绑定后有论文空间 | 10 epoch 内 K9 mAP 不涨 ≥0.4，或 bbox-jitter feature std 不降 ≥10%，或 STN 位移大量打满边界，杀 | **8/10**，若只有现成 crop 无原图/margin，降到 6.5 |
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train2_input.md:76:| **2. LAIR: lattice-aware learned resizer / anti-alias kernel** | resize kernel / subpixel sampling | 让 LR aliasing 对 phase 更稳定，同时保留衣服颜色、条纹、鞋包等细节 | learned resizer、anti-alias CNN 已有；ReID lattice-conditioned resizer 还有空间 | first eval K9 不涨 ≥0.3，或 kernel 高频能量爆、图像伪纹理明显，杀 | **7/10** |
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train2_input.md:77:| **3. bbox-aware adaptive padding/context crop** | crop margin/context | 如果 bbox 偏紧，动态引入上下文能减少头脚/边缘丢失 | 更像工程强对照，不够主贡献 | 对比固定 margin crop，K9 不涨 ≥0.3，杀 | 6/10，有原图更高 |
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train2_input.md:78:| **4. sampling-aware degradation generator** | 训练输入分布 | 生成更真实的 detector bbox + phase + kernel 退化，让 backbone 学到真实 LR 判别 cue | lattice aug 已死，只有“真实检测误差建模”才值得试 | 不显著 beat 普通 jitter/lattice aug，立即杀 | 4.5/10 |
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train2_input.md:79:| **5. polyphase/APS-style input stem** | phase selection/downsample | 减少 stride/patch embed 的 shift aliasing | anti-alias/APS 已有，且可能削弱 marginalization 残差信息 | phase variance 降但 K9 final 不涨，杀 | 5.5/10 |
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train2_input.md:83:先做 **BLC / LC-STN++**。它最贴“bbox 检测框主导”这个实测结论，且 kill-switch 便宜清楚。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train2_input.md:85:关键不要把目标写成“让所有 lattice feature 一致”。那条路你已经证伪过。目标应该是：
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train2_input.md:89:让 K=9 剩下的差异变成有意义的不确定性，而不是 bbox 噪声。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train2_input.md:92:learned resizer 放第二优先级。已有 learned image resizing 证明 task-driven resizer 可以优于 bilinear/bicubic，anti-aliased CNN/APS 也说明 downsample/phase 会影响 shift stability；但这些更像处理 sampling/kernel，不如 bbox canonicalizer 直打当前主因。([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/2103.09950)) ([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/1904.11486)) ([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/2011.14214))
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train2_input.md:94:CRReID 既有路线大量集中在 SR、multi-resolution representation、resolution-adaptive feature/mask/distillation，和你现在的“sampling-lattice latent + bbox uncertainty”不是同一个主问题。([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/1906.09748)) ([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/2105.12684)) ([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/2207.13037))
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train2_input.md:99:最贴你这个“bbox 检测框不确定性主导 +2.84”的发现的训练端 input-level 机制，不是 learned kernel，也不是普通 lattice aug，而是：

experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/novelty_rankinstab.md:14:我们要做 ReID **效率**方法 Rank-Instability Adaptive ReID。idea: ReID 默认所有 query 用 full 网络推理(uniform compute); 应 **rank-stability conditioned**——用 cheap(早层/低分辨率)特征算的 top-k 排名 vs full 排名的**不一致度(rank instability)** 当路由信号: 稳定 query 早退(cheap 够), 不稳定走 full。隐藏变量 = cheap-vs-full rank disagreement(不是一个静态 difficulty score)。效率-精度 Pareto。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/novelty_rankinstab.md:102:我们要做 ReID **效率**方法 Rank-Instability Adaptive ReID。idea: ReID 默认所有 query 用 full 网络推理(uniform compute); 应 **rank-stability conditioned**——用 cheap(早层/低分辨率)特征算的 top-k 排名 vs full 排名的**不一致度(rank instability)** 当路由信号: 稳定 query 早退(cheap 够), 不稳定走 full。隐藏变量 = cheap-vs-full rank disagreement(不是一个静态 difficulty score)。效率-精度 Pareto。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/novelty_rankinstab.md:349:./novelty_rankinstab.md:14:我们要做 ReID **效率**方法 Rank-Instability Adaptive ReID。idea: ReID 默认所有 query 用 full 网络推理(uniform compute); 应 **rank-stability conditioned**——用 cheap(早层/低分辨率)特征算的 top-k 排名 vs full 排名的**不一致度(rank instability)** 当路由信号: 稳定 query 早退(cheap 够), 不稳定走 full。隐藏变量 = cheap-vs-full rank disagreement(不是一个静态 difficulty score)。效率-精度 Pareto。

experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/novelty_rankinstab.md:5570:./reassess/r_2.md:1918:reassess/r_3.md:4490:/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/transfer_candidates.json:78:"transfer_to_occluded": "Recast the occluder as a confounder and apply the same backdoor-adjustment recipe: build an \"Occluder Dictionary\" of occlusion patterns/regions stored as Gaussians with priors P(occluder), then train with P(Y|do(X))=Σ_occluder P(Y|X,occluder)·P(occluder), marginalizing over occluders so the model cannot exploit occluder-identity spurious correlations (e.g. a particular bicycle/car/pole co-occurring with certain galleries or cameras). Two concretely transferable tricks: (1) the reverse-attention masking that isolates the confounder feature from the identity feature map — directly usable to separate occluder-region features from body features without external segmentation; (2) representing each confounder as a sampled Gaussian rather than a stored feature, giving cheap synthetic occluder augmentation at the feature level. The NWGM trick keeps the marginalization a single classification, so it's nearly free at training time and zero-cost at inference.",
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/novelty_rankinstab.md:5647:./reassess/r_2.md:4417:reassess/r_2.md:1918:reassess/r_3.md:4490:/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/transfer_candidates.json:78:"transfer_to_occluded": "Recast the occluder as a confounder and apply the same backdoor-adjustment recipe: build an \"Occluder Dictionary\" of occlusion patterns/regions stored as Gaussians with priors P(occluder), then train with P(Y|do(X))=Σ_occluder P(Y|X,occluder)·P(occluder), marginalizing over occluders so the model cannot exploit occluder-identity spurious correlations (e.g. a particular bicycle/car/pole co-occurring with certain galleries or cameras). Two concretely transferable tricks: (1) the reverse-attention masking that isolates the confounder feature from the identity feature map — directly usable to separate occluder-region features from body features without external segmentation; (2) representing each confounder as a sampled Gaussian rather than a stored feature, giving cheap synthetic occluder augmentation at the feature level. The NWGM trick keeps the marginalization a single classification, so it's nearly free at training time and zero-cost at inference.",

experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/reviews/deep_28.md:259:- A 语义/VLM teacher: 已完成,见 experiments/paper_materials/newinfo_semantic_teacher-codex.md。结论是遮挡语义 teacher 对 SOLIDER 强主干整体偏负,FLaN-Net/π-VL/AG-ReID/LVLM-ReID/RMPSNet 等发表遮挡成绩多低于或接近现有强基线; 唯一值得先探的是属性 teacher 的可靠性噪声屏蔽,其次是 teacher 只用于属性级样本组织,两者都必须先做零训练探针。

experiments/cargo_cvpb/airl_codex_bundle/reviews/codex_3.md:8469:web search: cross-resolution low-resolution person re-identification resolution invariance consistency degradation training
experiments/cargo_cvpb/airl_codex_bundle/reviews/codex_3.md:8529:专项风险是解释层面：这套机制更像“ground 低分辨率增强 + clean/degraded self-distillation + 双头 score fusion”，不是天然证明了 rec 学到非平凡 recover pole。cross-resolution ReID 里分辨率不变表征、超分/双流、低高分辨率互补早有先例；AG-ReID 近年也有 geometry/view synthesis/semantic alignment 路线。AIRL 若写论文，应把 novelty 收紧到“AG-ReID 中 ground-to-aerial pixel-budget degradation consistency + isolated dual-head fixed soft fusion”，不要泛称 resolution-consistency 本身新。参考：Cross-Resolution DAL、RAIN/Resolution-invariant ReID、近期 AG-ReID GIQT/SD-ReID/GSAlign，以及 KD/Mean Teacher 一致性范式。([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/1810.10221?utm_source=openai)) ([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/1907.10843?utm_source=openai)) ([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/1906.09748?utm_source=openai)) ([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21405?utm_source=openai)) ([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.09549?utm_source=openai)) ([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22268?utm_source=openai)) ([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/1503.02531?utm_source=openai))
experiments/cargo_cvpb/airl_codex_bundle/reviews/codex_3.md:8560:专项风险是解释层面：这套机制更像“ground 低分辨率增强 + clean/degraded self-distillation + 双头 score fusion”，不是天然证明了 rec 学到非平凡 recover pole。cross-resolution ReID 里分辨率不变表征、超分/双流、低高分辨率互补早有先例；AG-ReID 近年也有 geometry/view synthesis/semantic alignment 路线。AIRL 若写论文，应把 novelty 收紧到“AG-ReID 中 ground-to-aerial pixel-budget degradation consistency + isolated dual-head fixed soft fusion”，不要泛称 resolution-consistency 本身新。参考：Cross-Resolution DAL、RAIN/Resolution-invariant ReID、近期 AG-ReID GIQT/SD-ReID/GSAlign，以及 KD/Mean Teacher 一致性范式。([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/1810.10221?utm_source=openai)) ([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/1907.10843?utm_source=openai)) ([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/1906.09748?utm_source=openai)) ([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21405?utm_source=openai)) ([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.09549?utm_source=openai)) ([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22268?utm_source=openai)) ([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/1503.02531?utm_source=openai))
./experiments/POSE_INTO_CLIP_summary.md:35:- 外挂式 (姿态当独立描述子, 不碰对齐): +0.2 mAP / +0.5 R1, **seed 噪声内, marginal**。
./experiments/POSE_INTO_CLIP_summary.md:36:- **Step1 CLIP 机制 (+2.2) 是真贡献; 姿态在其上最多 marginal, 且必须"外挂不碰对齐"**。用户路线: Step1 成, Step2 系统证明姿态加不动 (整合害, 外挂微涨)。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/reviews/deep_9.md:823:- A 语义/VLM teacher: 已完成,见 experiments/paper_materials/newinfo_semantic_teacher-codex.md。结论是遮挡语义 teacher 对 SOLIDER 强主干整体偏负,FLaN-Net/π-VL/AG-ReID/LVLM-ReID/RMPSNet 等发表遮挡成绩多低于或接近现有强基线; 唯一值得先探的是属性 teacher 的可靠性噪声屏蔽,其次是 teacher 只用于属性级样本组织,两者都必须先做零训练探针。

./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train3_fourthclass.md:4806:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/false_negative_audit.md:12282:/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/validate/v_2.md:14529:   预测/估计的 `σ_A` 必须系统性大于 `σ_G`，并且和物理指标相关：aerial bbox area 小、投影人体像素少、头肩俯视比例高、blur/downsample 强时方差更大。

./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train3_fourthclass.md:4809:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/false_negative_audit.md:12649:/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/GOPL_KILLSWITCH_DESIGN.md:35:**通过标准:** (核心1-4成立) + (D1置换破) + (D2 SMPL>bbox/cam) + (D3 SMPL beyond feature-dist) + **(D4 cov3d>cov2d 明显)**。D4 不过 = GOPL 没独特性, 降级。全过 = GOPL 隐藏变量真实且 SMPL 几何独特, 写训练版(geometry-ordered positive graph + 课程权重, beat random/self-paced/feature-hard/2D-vis 四对照)。

./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train3_fourthclass.md:4923:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/false_negative_audit.md:17623:/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/GOPL_KILLSWITCH_DESIGN.md:23:1. **同 ID pair 距离 vs overlap**: 所有 same-ID pair, frozen cosine 距离 vs `1-cov`。Spearman, 控遮挡程度(可见关节数)/bbox。预期: cov3d 的负相关显著, 且强于 cov2d。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train3_fourthclass.md:4924:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/false_negative_audit.md:17625:/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/GOPL_KILLSWITCH_DESIGN.md:35:**通过标准:** (核心1-4成立) + (D1置换破) + (D2 SMPL>bbox/cam) + (D3 SMPL beyond feature-dist) + **(D4 cov3d>cov2d 明显)**。D4 不过 = GOPL 没独特性, 降级。全过 = GOPL 隐藏变量真实且 SMPL 几何独特, 写训练版(geometry-ordered positive graph + 课程权重, beat random/self-paced/feature-hard/2D-vis 四对照)。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train3_fourthclass.md:4925:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/false_negative_audit.md:17699:/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/novelty_rankinstab.md:5570:./reassess/r_2.md:1918:reassess/r_3.md:4490:/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/transfer_candidates.json:78:"transfer_to_occluded": "Recast the occluder as a confounder and apply the same backdoor-adjustment recipe: build an \"Occluder Dictionary\" of occlusion patterns/regions stored as Gaussians with priors P(occluder), then train with P(Y|do(X))=Σ_occluder P(Y|X,occluder)·P(occluder), marginalizing over occluders so the model cannot exploit occluder-identity spurious correlations (e.g. a particular bicycle/car/pole co-occurring with certain galleries or cameras). Two concretely transferable tricks: (1) the reverse-attention masking that isolates the confounder feature from the identity feature map — directly usable to separate occluder-region features from body features without external segmentation; (2) representing each confounder as a sampled Gaussian rather than a stored feature, giving cheap synthetic occluder augmentation at the feature level. The NWGM trick keeps the marginalization a single classification, so it's nearly free at training time and zero-cost at inference.",
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train3_fourthclass.md:4926:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/false_negative_audit.md:17708:/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/novelty_rankinstab.md:5647:./reassess/r_2.md:4417:reassess/r_2.md:1918:reassess/r_3.md:4490:/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/transfer_candidates.json:78:"transfer_to_occluded": "Recast the occluder as a confounder and apply the same backdoor-adjustment recipe: build an \"Occluder Dictionary\" of occlusion patterns/regions stored as Gaussians with priors P(occluder), then train with P(Y|do(X))=Σ_occluder P(Y|X,occluder)·P(occluder), marginalizing over occluders so the model cannot exploit occluder-identity spurious correlations (e.g. a particular bicycle/car/pole co-occurring with certain galleries or cameras). Two concretely transferable tricks: (1) the reverse-attention masking that isolates the confounder feature from the identity feature map — directly usable to separate occluder-region features from body features without external segmentation; (2) representing each confounder as a sampled Gaussian rather than a stored feature, giving cheap synthetic occluder augmentation at the feature level. The NWGM trick keeps the marginalization a single classification, so it's nearly free at training time and zero-cost at inference.",

./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train3_fourthclass.md:4967:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/false_negative_audit.md:18137:/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/false_negative_audit.md:418:**通过标准:** (核心1-4成立) + (D1置换破) + (D2 SMPL>bbox/cam) + (D3 SMPL beyond feature-dist) + **(D4 cov3d>cov2d 明显)**。D4 不过 = GOPL 没独特性, 降级。全过 = GOPL 隐藏变量真实且 SMPL 几何独特, 写训练版(geometry-ordered positive graph + 课程权重, beat random/self-paced/feature-hard/2D-vis 四对照)。

./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train3_fourthclass.md:4977:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/false_negative_audit.md:18809:/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/false_negative_audit.md:10629:/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/novelty_rankinstab.md:5570:./reassess/r_2.md:1918:reassess/r_3.md:4490:/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/transfer_candidates.json:78:"transfer_to_occluded": "Recast the occluder as a confounder and apply the same backdoor-adjustment recipe: build an \"Occluder Dictionary\" of occlusion patterns/regions stored as Gaussians with priors P(occluder), then train with P(Y|do(X))=Σ_occluder P(Y|X,occluder)·P(occluder), marginalizing over occluders so the model cannot exploit occluder-identity spurious correlations (e.g. a particular bicycle/car/pole co-occurring with certain galleries or cameras). Two concretely transferable tricks: (1) the reverse-attention masking that isolates the confounder feature from the identity feature map — directly usable to separate occluder-region features from body features without external segmentation; (2) representing each confounder as a sampled Gaussian rather than a stored feature, giving cheap synthetic occluder augmentation at the feature level. The NWGM trick keeps the marginalization a single classification, so it's nearly free at training time and zero-cost at inference.",

./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train3_fourthclass.md:5059:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/analyses/ALL_ANALYSES.txt:763:3. **机制怎么长出来**：既然不同 Transformer 层看不同内容，就用 MLDC 给 12 层动态加权，并用层间相关惩罚减少冗余。既然全局层融合会丢局部，就用 LPAM 选第 3、6、9、12 层做金字塔式局部融合。既然 gait 和结构细节可能体现在频域，就把 FFT 放进 self-attention 里做频域相关。训练上只用 ID loss、triplet loss 和自适应 loss 权重。机制和“多层多尺度”绑定较紧，和“FFT 捕捉步态结构”的绑定稍弱，需要更多专门证据。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train3_fourthclass.md:5060:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/analyses/ALL_ANALYSES.txt:765:4. **证据闭环**：Table 7 证明 MLDC、LPAM、两者组合和 FFT 逐级带来增益，而且在多个换衣数据集上都有趋势。层选择实验说明 3、6、9、12 覆盖低中高层最优；自适应 loss 权重优于手调固定权重；t-SNE 和检索可视化显示同身份聚得更紧。VC-Clothes-W&R 上也有 SOTA 结果，但作者自己承认雨风场景下降幅度不一定比别人小，所以“天气鲁棒”不是最强闭环，“RGB-only 多层多尺度换衣识别”才是主闭环。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train3_fourthclass.md:5065:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/analyses/ALL_ANALYSES.txt:1403:它把 LVLM 从“生成描述的外部工具”改写成“产生身份语义 token 的特征提取器组成部分”。关键词是 LVLM-ReID、Pedestrian Semantic Token Generation、`<REID>` token、Semantic-Guided Interaction、reinforced semantic token、generative language models assist discriminative visual models。旧问题是“怎么给 ReID 加文本语义”，新问题是“怎样把 LVLM 的生成过程变成 ReID 的身份表示”。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train3_fourthclass.md:5068:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/clean/d_20.txt:1:按 2026-06-25 联网核查，我不建议再碰 open-world/DG、promptable bbox、航拍-地面、occluded cloth-changing：OWD 2024 已做 open-world benchmark，KPR 2024 已做 multi-person bbox ambiguity，AG-ReID.v2 已占航拍-地面，OC4-ReID 2024 已做遮挡+换衣组合。下面两个更像可构造的新协议。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train3_fourthclass.md:5069:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/clean/d_20.txt:16:**撞车核查**：老的 set-based ReID 确实处理过低质量/语义噪声图像集，但主要是视频/set 表征质量问题，不是“测试时弱 query bag、未知目标纯度、多假设检索”协议；2019 ID-aware Quality 还明确以 set-to-set 和质量权重为核心。KPR 2024 处理的是同一 bbox 多人歧义，不是跨图片 query-bag 污染。  
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train3_fourthclass.md:5071:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/clean/d_12.txt:12:做 `Camera-Pair Risk Equalization`。训练时把源域 camera-pair 当 episode domain，leave-one-camera-pair-out 做 meta-test；优化 worst camera-pair loss。另加一个轻量 pair-risk predictor，用 source camera-pair 的颜色、亮度、bbox ratio、view proxy、feature centroid distance 估计风险，训练时对高风险 pair 加权或做 pair-style counterfactual augmentation。全程不碰 target，不走 rerank。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train3_fourthclass.md:5072:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/clean/d_12.txt:14:廉价 kill-switch：零训练。用现有 frozen SOLIDER/exp030a 特征，在 Market/MSMT/CARGO/AG-ReID.v2 上按 camera-pair 分桶算 AP/R1；同时算 `pair_shift_score = camera style distance + bbox/crop/view distance + source pair coverage distance`。  
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train3_fourthclass.md:5074:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/clean/d_8.txt:1:结论先说：低/跨分辨率 ReID 不是完全空白，但“常规 CR-ReID”已经偏红海。不要做 SR、resolution-invariant/adaptive mask、全局 HR-LR feature offset、CLIP/VLM multi-scenario。2025/2026 的 VPFA 已经把“分辨率是特征空间方向”讲掉了；2024-2026 还有 VersReID、ITKM 这类多场景/CLIP 框架覆盖 low-resolution 场景；更早 LRAR/RFD/MRJL 已覆盖动态 metric、多分辨率 gallery、SR-free/adaptive 表征。可争的机会必须从“图像形成过程”或“LR 下监督定义错误”切进去，而不是再做特征补偿。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train3_fourthclass.md:5075:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/clean/d_8.txt:6:触发观察/失败：现有 CR-ReID 默认 LR 是 HR crop 经一次确定性 bicubic/downsample 得到，bbox 完美、采样相位固定。但真实低分辨率里，一个人高只有 16-32 px 时，±1 个 LR 像素 bbox 偏移或 0.25/0.5 像素 sub-pixel phase 就会改变衣服条纹、鞋/腿、包带是否被采到。大家以为问题是“分辨率低，细节缺失”；其实可能是“同一个人对应一族 alias/crop-lattice 观测，模型只见过其中一个”。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train3_fourthclass.md:5076:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/clean/d_8.txt:8:重定义：Cross-resolution ReID = lattice-uncertain observation problem。隐藏变量不是 resolution ratio，而是采样相位、bbox 量化、downsample kernel、轻微 detector crop error。身份匹配应对这族观测做边缘化，而不是把某个确定性 LR 图当真值。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train3_fourthclass.md:5077:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/clean/d_8.txt:13:- 训练端做 phase-consistency / phase-adversarial augmentation，让同一 HR 图在不同 LR lattice 下保持身份稳定。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train3_fourthclass.md:5078:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/clean/d_8.txt:18:2. 每张 query 生成 9 或 25 个 phase/bbox variants：subpixel shift、±1 LR pixel crop shift/expand、不同 antialias kernel。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train3_fourthclass.md:5079:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/clean/d_8.txt:26:撞车核查：我没查到 2024-2026 直接做 “subpixel/crop lattice uncertainty for low-resolution ReID” 的工作。VPFA 是 feature-space resolution direction，不是采样相位；LRAR 是 resolution-adaptive representation/mask；RFD 处理 multi-resolution gallery 与 feature distillation；VersReID/ITKM 是多场景/CLIP 框架。这个点目前不像红海，但要继续查 “aliasing / anti-alias / bbox jitter / sub-pixel ReID”。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train3_fourthclass.md:5080:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/clean/d_8.txt:54:参考核查：VPFA 2025/2026 做 resolution direction 与 post-hoc feature panning；VersReID 2024 覆盖 multi-scene including low-resolution；ITKM 2026 做 unsupervised multi-scenario + CLIP；LRAR 2022 做 resolution-adaptive representation/masks；RFD 2021 处理 multi-resolution gallery/feature distillation。  
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train3_fourthclass.md:5082:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/d_20.md:52:初筛里已有撞车信号：open-world/open-set、lifelong/re-index-free、航拍-地面、occlusion/promptable bbox、低清/跨分辨率都已经有近年论文或挑战，不能直接当新坑。下一步我把“可构造、能零训练验尸、且不落入这些红海”的候选收紧。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/train3_fourthclass.md:5108:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/d_20.md:844:| 157d-eq | Body-random occlusion (eq_concat) | 61.0% | 71.5% | 84.4% | 88.4% | 🟡 中性偏负 vs baseline: -0.1/-2.2。人体 bbox 随机遮挡不优于 ROA |

./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/analyses/ALL_ANALYSES.txt:1249:1. 触发观察：大多数 ReID 研究默认固定地面摄像头，但 UAV 有高空视角、动态背景、运动模糊、低分辨率、续航和算力约束。作者还指出 UAV-ReID 数据集非常少，和普通 ReID 数据集规模不成比例。

./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/analyses/ALL_ANALYSES.txt:1821:1. 触发观察：AGPReID 里航拍和地面视角差异极大。属性方法依赖人工或检测属性，但航拍低分辨率下属性不可靠；视角解耦方法看似合理，却可能把身体轮廓、结构过渡这类身份相关但带视角性的线索一起删掉。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/analyses/deep_2.txt:69:3. **机制怎么长出来**：既然不同 Transformer 层看不同内容，就用 MLDC 给 12 层动态加权，并用层间相关惩罚减少冗余。既然全局层融合会丢局部，就用 LPAM 选第 3、6、9、12 层做金字塔式局部融合。既然 gait 和结构细节可能体现在频域，就把 FFT 放进 self-attention 里做频域相关。训练上只用 ID loss、triplet loss 和自适应 loss 权重。机制和“多层多尺度”绑定较紧，和“FFT 捕捉步态结构”的绑定稍弱，需要更多专门证据。

./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/d_18.md:44:web search: 2024 gait recognition low resolution 2D skeleton sequence surveillance
./experiments/transfer_candidates.json:78:"transfer_to_occluded": "Recast the occluder as a confounder and apply the same backdoor-adjustment recipe: build an \"Occluder Dictionary\" of occlusion patterns/regions stored as Gaussians with priors P(occluder), then train with P(Y|do(X))=Σ_occluder P(Y|X,occluder)·P(occluder), marginalizing over occluders so the model cannot exploit occluder-identity spurious correlations (e.g. a particular bicycle/car/pole co-occurring with certain galleries or cameras). Two concretely transferable tricks: (1) the reverse-attention masking that isolates the confounder feature from the identity feature map — directly usable to separate occluder-region features from body features without external segmentation; (2) representing each confounder as a sampled Gaussian rather than a stored feature, giving cheap synthetic occluder augmentation at the feature level. The NWGM trick keeps the marginalization a single classification, so it's nearly free at training time and zero-cost at inference.",
./experiments/transfer_candidates.json:80:"novel_component": "The NWGM-approximated backdoor adjustment implemented as a feature-level Confounder Dictionary: each nuisance factor is stored as an EMA-updated Gaussian N(μ,σ²) with a prior, K samples are drawn and condition-injected into the identity feature, and the classifier is moved outside the Σ_c sum (NWGM) so marginalizing over the confounder costs one forward pass — turning the abstract \"intervene over all confounder values\" into a cheap, train-only operation that disappears at inference."
./experiments/exp170/design.md:46:- 用 keypoints 坐标计算身体部位 bbox（带 padding 扩展）
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/analyses/deep_26.txt:53:1. 触发观察：大多数 ReID 研究默认固定地面摄像头，但 UAV 有高空视角、动态背景、运动模糊、低分辨率、续航和算力约束。作者还指出 UAV-ReID 数据集非常少，和普通 ReID 数据集规模不成比例。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/B_CONTAINMENT_DESIGN.md:28:**隐藏变量(可测、可证伪)**: 视角成像导致的**信息欠定度不对称** σ_aerial ≫ σ_ground, 且 σ 由物理量(bbox 面积/SMPL 投影身体像素/俯视比/分辨率)决定, 不是难度代理。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/B_CONTAINMENT_DESIGN.md:45:- 诊断: trace(σ_A) 显著 > trace(σ_G); σ 与 bbox面积↓/SMPL投影像素↓/分辨率↓ 相关。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/B_CONTAINMENT_DESIGN.md:59:8. 收益集中度: 按 SMPL投影像素/bbox面积/分辨率分 4 桶, 最小航拍桶包含收益最大, 高清/同视角弱→全桶平均涨=普通 metric trick。
./experiments/exp334_smpl_geom/design.md:26:- 失败最可能：① 遮挡处 attend 到遮挡物（location≠visibility）抵消增益；② 弱 baseline 已靠 bbox crop 聚焦人体，body 先验冗余。
./experiments/exp170/claude_review.md:12:- 边界条件处理：<2 visible keypoints 跳过，bbox <5px 跳过，无 occluders fallback
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/clean/d_14.txt:1:结论先放前面：**CLIP/DINO/SAM 直接当 backbone、prompt learner、text anchor、SAM 遮挡增强、LVLM 生成语义 token 这条线基本红了**。2024-2026 已经有 CLIP 语义注入 VI-ReID、CLIP selective prompt tuning、LVLM-ReID、LLaVA/MLLM interactive ReID、SAM occlusion augmentation、KPR promptable ReID 等工作在占位。继续“导入 FM 提精度”不建议碰。参考：CSDN/CLIP-VIReID 已做语义桥接与 prompt learner([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.05806))，SCING 做 selective cross-modal prompt tuning([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.00506))，LVLM-ReID 做 LVLM semantic token([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.18111))，LLaVA-ReID/ICL 已做交互式文本 ReID([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.10174))，DPEFormer 已用 SAM 做 realistic occlusion augmentation([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.10435))。

./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/clean/d_14.txt:21:触发观察：KPR 2024 已明确指出 multi-person ambiguity：一个 ReID bbox 里可能有多个人，目标身份在像素层面不明确，并用 keypoint prompt 指定目标([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.18112))。这说明传统 ReID 的 “bbox = target person” 假设是假的。但 KPR 已经很近，不能直接复刻 keypoint prompt。

./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/d_20.md:976:按 2026-06-25 联网核查，我不建议再碰 open-world/DG、promptable bbox、航拍-地面、occluded cloth-changing：OWD 2024 已做 open-world benchmark，KPR 2024 已做 multi-person bbox ambiguity，AG-ReID.v2 已占航拍-地面，OC4-ReID 2024 已做遮挡+换衣组合。下面两个更像可构造的新协议。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/d_20.md:991:**撞车核查**：老的 set-based ReID 确实处理过低质量/语义噪声图像集，但主要是视频/set 表征质量问题，不是“测试时弱 query bag、未知目标纯度、多假设检索”协议；2019 ID-aware Quality 还明确以 set-to-set 和质量权重为核心。KPR 2024 处理的是同一 bbox 多人歧义，不是跨图片 query-bag 污染。  
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/d_20.md:1014:按 2026-06-25 联网核查，我不建议再碰 open-world/DG、promptable bbox、航拍-地面、occluded cloth-changing：OWD 2024 已做 open-world benchmark，KPR 2024 已做 multi-person bbox ambiguity，AG-ReID.v2 已占航拍-地面，OC4-ReID 2024 已做遮挡+换衣组合。下面两个更像可构造的新协议。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/d_20.md:1029:**撞车核查**：老的 set-based ReID 确实处理过低质量/语义噪声图像集，但主要是视频/set 表征质量问题，不是“测试时弱 query bag、未知目标纯度、多假设检索”协议；2019 ID-aware Quality 还明确以 set-to-set 和质量权重为核心。KPR 2024 处理的是同一 bbox 多人歧义，不是跨图片 query-bag 污染。  
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/clean/d_8.txt:1:结论先说：低/跨分辨率 ReID 不是完全空白，但“常规 CR-ReID”已经偏红海。不要做 SR、resolution-invariant/adaptive mask、全局 HR-LR feature offset、CLIP/VLM multi-scenario。2025/2026 的 VPFA 已经把“分辨率是特征空间方向”讲掉了；2024-2026 还有 VersReID、ITKM 这类多场景/CLIP 框架覆盖 low-resolution 场景；更早 LRAR/RFD/MRJL 已覆盖动态 metric、多分辨率 gallery、SR-free/adaptive 表征。可争的机会必须从“图像形成过程”或“LR 下监督定义错误”切进去，而不是再做特征补偿。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/clean/d_8.txt:3:**机会 1：低分辨率不是模糊图，而是采样格点不确定性**
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/clean/d_8.txt:6:触发观察/失败：现有 CR-ReID 默认 LR 是 HR crop 经一次确定性 bicubic/downsample 得到，bbox 完美、采样相位固定。但真实低分辨率里，一个人高只有 16-32 px 时，±1 个 LR 像素 bbox 偏移或 0.25/0.5 像素 sub-pixel phase 就会改变衣服条纹、鞋/腿、包带是否被采到。大家以为问题是“分辨率低，细节缺失”；其实可能是“同一个人对应一族 alias/crop-lattice 观测，模型只见过其中一个”。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/clean/d_8.txt:8:重定义：Cross-resolution ReID = lattice-uncertain observation problem。隐藏变量不是 resolution ratio，而是采样相位、bbox 量化、downsample kernel、轻微 detector crop error。身份匹配应对这族观测做边缘化，而不是把某个确定性 LR 图当真值。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/clean/d_8.txt:13:- 训练端做 phase-consistency / phase-adversarial augmentation，让同一 HR 图在不同 LR lattice 下保持身份稳定。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/clean/d_8.txt:18:2. 每张 query 生成 9 或 25 个 phase/bbox variants：subpixel shift、±1 LR pixel crop shift/expand、不同 antialias kernel。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/clean/d_8.txt:26:撞车核查：我没查到 2024-2026 直接做 “subpixel/crop lattice uncertainty for low-resolution ReID” 的工作。VPFA 是 feature-space resolution direction，不是采样相位；LRAR 是 resolution-adaptive representation/mask；RFD 处理 multi-resolution gallery 与 feature distillation；VersReID/ITKM 是多场景/CLIP 框架。这个点目前不像红海，但要继续查 “aliasing / anti-alias / bbox jitter / sub-pixel ReID”。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/clean/d_8.txt:35:重定义：Low-res ReID 不是“恢复缺失细节后做 ID 分类”，而是“给定分辨率 r，身份标签退化为观测等价类”。高分辨率身份是细粒度标签；低分辨率只能监督 coarse identity evidence。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/clean/d_8.txt:40:- 约束表示具备嵌套性：低分辨率特征只学所有 resolution 共享信息，高分辨率再细化身份。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/clean/d_8.txt:50:撞车核查：LRAR 已经有“按 query resolution 比较共享子向量”的相近思想，所以这条不能只讲 shared information；必须讲“监督标签在低分辨率下退化/negative label relaxation”。目前我没看到 2024-2026 直接以 “resolution-induced label collapse / observational equivalence classes” 为主问题的 CR-ReID 方法，但边界比机会 1 更窄。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/clean/d_8.txt:54:参考核查：VPFA 2025/2026 做 resolution direction 与 post-hoc feature panning；VersReID 2024 覆盖 multi-scene including low-resolution；ITKM 2026 做 unsupervised multi-scenario + CLIP；LRAR 2022 做 resolution-adaptive representation/masks；RFD 2021 处理 multi-resolution gallery/feature distillation。  
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/clean/d_20.txt:1:按 2026-06-25 联网核查，我不建议再碰 open-world/DG、promptable bbox、航拍-地面、occluded cloth-changing：OWD 2024 已做 open-world benchmark，KPR 2024 已做 multi-person bbox ambiguity，AG-ReID.v2 已占航拍-地面，OC4-ReID 2024 已做遮挡+换衣组合。下面两个更像可构造的新协议。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/explore20/clean/d_20.txt:16:**撞车核查**：老的 set-based ReID 确实处理过低质量/语义噪声图像集，但主要是视频/set 表征质量问题，不是“测试时弱 query bag、未知目标纯度、多假设检索”协议；2019 ID-aware Quality 还明确以 set-to-set 和质量权重为核心。KPR 2024 处理的是同一 bbox 多人歧义，不是跨图片 query-bag 污染。  
./experiments/overnight_innovation_log.md:51:3. **Frozen partial/unbalanced-OT**：把 part-MaxSim 的平均换成 5×5 Sinkhorn（可见性当 marginal，occlusion=mass destruction）。angle-4 kill-switch。
