./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review.md:1:# Codex Review — exp359 LM-ReID
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review.md:8:- **High-1** [line 173]: 每图 RNG `seed+idx` → 每 epoch 同一组 lattice variants（无多样性，废掉 lattice-invariance 学习）。**修**: `LatticeTrainSet.set_epoch(ep)` 每 epoch 调 + seed 混 epoch（`seed = cli.seed*1000003 + epoch*9973 + idx*2654435761`）；DataLoader persistent_workers 默认 False → worker 每 epoch 重 fork 看新 epoch。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review.md:9:- **High-2** [line 48/118]: M=2+`j%3` 只训 canonical+bbox（没训 phase/zoom/kernel）。**修**: `make_lattice_variants(rand_mode=True)` 每非 canonical variant 随机 axis+kernel；返回 (variants, axes)；M 默认→3。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review.md:12:- **L_adv 真 axis**: disc 输出→4（axes 0=canon/1=phase/2=bbox/3=zoom）；`axis_lbl=axb.reshape(B*M)` image-major 对齐 gf。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review.md:22:- Medium-4 resolved: triplet per lattice slot over [B,D] with labels y, averaged over M.
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/claude_review.md:1:# Claude Broad Review — exp359 LM-ReID 训练脚本
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/claude_review.md:8:- **不是小调参**：LM-ReID 是新机制（lattice-marginalized embedding），重定义低分辨率 ReID = 采样格点不确定性；有零训练 kill-switch GO（独立轴、双 trivial 控制 vs-TTA/vs-#false 都过、GO 靠 interventional 直接测量）做前置，codex 判 CCF-B 7/10。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/claude_review.md:9:- **单变量**：唯一变量 = "训练 lattice-invariance" vs "frozen 模型"。eval 复用 kill-switch（byte-identical degradation），与 frozen baseline 只差"是否 fine-tune"。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/claude_review.md:23:- **L_adv** = GRL：disc 预测 variant slot，梯度反转使 z 不含可预测 lattice label。`slot=arange(M).repeat(B)` 与 image-major reshape 对齐。warmup-gated（adv_start），默认 lam_adv=0（弱辅助先不开，可后续 ablation）。✓
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/claude_review.md:29:- lattice 生成函数（make_lr/make_lattice_variants/pil_to_tensor_np/_to_target_aspect）**逐行复制自 cvpb_lattice_killswitch.py** → 训练降质与 GO kill-switch eval **byte-identical**（单变量隔离的关键）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/claude_review.md:43:逐行审查 + 源码接口验证完成，4 处问题已修（KL 方向 / AMP / 稳健 out 取值 / scaler backward）。代码正确、对照隔离干净（lattice 生成 byte-identical 复用、eval 复用 kill-switch）、过线判据明确、诚实判死路径清楚。**审查通过**，可进 smoke + 训练（仍需 Codex 独立审通过）。
./experiments/paper_notes/paper_17_DPEFormer.md:67:2. 训练时随机选取一个 mask，缩放到占行人 bbox 面积的 50-75%
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_options.md:1:# LM-ReID 训练端创新候选（2026-06-26，3-codex 头脑风暴综合）
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_options.md:6:训练端做 invariance / feature-collapse 压判别性 = 死路：consistency −1.73、lattice-aug 不特殊 +0.54、L_marg 训练有害大元凶。新机制必须绕开"逼 K 变体特征塌成一个点"。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_options.md:8:## 机制 A：学怎么加权边缘化（LPA / LUD）— codex 最高 7.5/10
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_options.md:9:- **LPA（Lattice Posterior Amortization，lens3 #1）**：冻结 backbone，只训小头预测 posterior qφ(z|x)（z=bbox/phase/kernel 格点），测试时 `score = logsumexp_z[log qφ(z|q) + cos(fθ(Tz q), fθ(g))/τ]`，uniform → posterior 加权。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_options.md:10:- **LUD（Lattice Uncertainty Distillation，lens2）= 同族**：训头预测 lattice 风险 risk_k（stopgrad teacher 的 spread + margin），测试 per-variant 加权。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_options.md:13:- **kill-switch**：冻 no-LM-loss ckpt + 训 qφ 1-3 epoch；weighted 比 uniform 多 ≥+0.4 mAP(h12/16) + 格点预测 acc ≥35% + LATTICE−TTA 不降 → 活，否则杀。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_options.md:14:- **不撞 PFE**：PFE=generic data-quality uncertainty；LPA=supervised sampling-lattice posterior（隐变量来源明确 = LR 采样格点，监督来自 K-hypothesis empirical spread）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_options.md:16:## 机制 B：学怎么对齐检测框（LC-STN / BLC）— 7/10（lens1）/ 6.5/10（lens3）
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_options.md:17:- 训小模块**估计 LR crop 格点偏移 (dx,dy) 并 grid_sample 重采样到 canonical**（类 STN，但监督几何参数非身份特征），残差留 test-time marginalization。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_options.md:18:- 第一版 translation-only sub-pixel re-centering（max_shift=1.25 LR px，tanh bound），只打 bbox 主因子。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_options.md:22:- **novelty**：STN/AlignedReID/PAN/STNReID/CDPM 先例多 → 卖点不是"用 STN"，是"LR detector crop lattice 当可边缘化隐变量 + 监督 canonicalization 非 invariance + 残差 decision marginalization"组合无先例。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_options.md:25:- raw Hard-Lattice ERM/CVaR（太像更狠 lattice-aug，续压判别性）→ 只做 cheap ablation（= 正在跑的 LM-S5，验证后即停）
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_options.md:29:- 两个 kill-switch 都冻结 backbone、廉价 → **两个都验，先 A（置信高）后 B（分量大）**。过的那个 = 训练端第二 contribution，和 test-time marginalization 组成完整 train+test 方法（推 7-8/10）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_options.md:31:- 当前 GPU 被 Hard-Lattice(3090)/Hard-ordinary(4090) 占 → 先实现两个 kill-switch 代码，GPU 一空即 cheap probe（不直接堆训练）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:14:Review the LM-ReID fine-tuning script: the single file matching experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lm_reid_*.py (exp359; design at experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md). 它 fine-tune frozen SOLIDER PoseBackboneModel(pose_dict=None → 纯 backbone global feat)成 lattice-marginalized embedding 抗采样格点(sub-pixel phase / +-1 LR-px bbox / antialias kernel)。请先 cat 该文件 + model/pose_backbone_model.py 的 forward。逐行审:
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:23:(9) 对照隔离: lattice 生成复制自 cvpb_lattice_killswitch.py(byte-identical 降质), eval 复用 kill-switch.
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:24:也用 web search 查 lattice-marginalization / sub-pixel sampling uncertainty for low-res ReID 是否有 exact 先例。输出: Verdict (approve / needs-attention) + findings (severity Critical/High/Medium/Low, 每条带行号+修法).
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:30:# 实验 exp359: LM-ReID (Lattice-Marginalized ReID)
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:32:> 后 PRCV「换问题」阶段第一个 GO 的 method 候选（9 个零训练 cheap-kill 全死后）。零训练 kill-switch 已 GO（d8 lattice, agent a68e6），method-design codex 判 **CCF-B 7/10**（训练版超 frozen ensemble → 8/10）。本实验做训练版，证明 lattice-marginalization 是 **method 不是 ensemble trick**。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:33:> 设计来源：`experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt`（完整 codex 设计）+ `cvpb_lattice_result.md`（kill-switch 数据）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:37:低分辨率 ReID 的传统视角：LR = 模糊/缺细节，解法 = SR / resolution-invariant feature。**我们重定义**：一部分 LR 失败不是"缺信息"，而是 **采样格点不确定性（sampling-lattice uncertainty）**——同一个 HR 身份在不同合法的 LR 采样格点（sub-pixel phase / bbox alignment / downsample kernel）下，落到不同 embedding 区域，导致 rank-1 身份翻转。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:40:frozen exp260b Market，K=9 lattice variants ensemble，HR gallery / LR query：
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:49:两个生死控制都过：**vs 普通 TTA**（同 K 同 MaxSim，lattice 比 TTA 多 +3.04，证 lattice-specific 非 TTA 换名；lat-MaxSim 46.9 > tta 43.8 > single 42.6，无"输给 trivial baseline"问题）；**vs #false-in-topk**（phase-var partial +0.19/+0.21 存活）。机制：phase-var 是 TTA-var 的 **7.5×**；74.9% h=16 query 跨 lattice 翻转 rank-1，随 h 升单调衰减到 1.2%（h=48 机制消失 = 低分辨率专用现象）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:52:phase-var 作 per-query 失败预测器**不干净**（控 LR-severity 后 partial 塌到 ≈0，与 per-image LR 失真共线）。**GO 靠的是 interventional 结果**（ensemble +4.2 / LAT−TTA +3.0 是直接测量）。故事写成 "lattice sensitivity 是 **mechanism-level nuisance**, 不是 standalone failure predictor"，方法是对所有 severe-LR query 做 marginalization（不是预测哪张失败）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:56:训练一个 lattice-marginalized embedding（对 lattice variants 身份稳定）+ 推理 K-marginalization，在 h=16 上比 frozen lattice ensemble **再高 +0.8~2.0 mAP** → 证明它学到了 lattice-invariance（是 method），不是 ensemble trick。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:62:2. fine-tune：HR train image 在线生成 LR lattice variants `x^l = U(D_l(x))`，l ∈ {sub-pixel phase, bbox jitter, downsample kernel}。每图每 iter 采样 M=2-4 variants，eval 用 K=9。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:65:### LM-ReID loss
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:69:L_marg = -log[ mean_l p^l[y] ] + Triplet(z^μ, y)                 # marginal likelihood（主贡献）
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:71:L_adv  = GRL-CE(Dφ(z^l), lattice_label_l)                        # 弱：去掉 embedding 中可预测 lattice label
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:76:### 推理 K-marginalization
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:87:- h=32：允许 marginal 不负。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:89:失败最可能原因：训练版只 ≈ frozen ensemble（没学到额外 lattice-invariance）→ 沦为 test-time ensemble trick，不成方法稿。备选投稿角度：同等 mAP 下 K 从 9 降到 3 或 single inference 保留大部分收益。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:95:- **frozen lattice ensemble**（零训练 K=9，= kill-switch 的 +4.23，这是训练版必须超过的硬线）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:98:消融：marg only / marg+cons / marg+cons+adv；τ sweep；K=1/3/5/9 曲线；phase-only vs +bbox+kernel。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:102:- 合成：Market/MSMT，gallery HR，query LR h=16/24/32，canonical LR single baseline，K=9（3×3 phase 主，bbox/kernel ablation，不无限扩 K）。所有 TTA 对照 **K-matched**。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:104:- 新指标：PRF@1（phase rank-flip rate）、Flip Entropy、LEG（lattice ensemble gain）、LOTG（lattice-over-TTA gain）、query ΔAP。按 h 分报 + paired bootstrap 95% CI + K=1/3/5/9 曲线 + compute cost。PRF 随 h（74.9%→31%→10%→1.2%）是强故事线。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:108:- **VPFA**（2510.00936，最近邻）：CR-ReID = feature-space resolution direction，Vector Panning LR→pseudo-HR。hidden variable 是 resolution gap/feature direction，**不是 sampling lattice，不做 lattice marginalization**。必须正面对比。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:109:- LRAR（2207.13037）：resolution-adaptive representation，占"resolution adaptive"词 → 我们 novelty 写 sampling-lattice uncertainty。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:110:- RFD（2109.07871）：multi-res gallery distillation，占"resolution-invariant distillation"，没占 lattice intervention。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:111:- BlurPool（1904.11486）：anti-aliasing/shift-invariance 已老 → **不能说首次发现 aliasing 影响**。**能声称**：首次在 LR person ReID 把采样格点作隐藏变量 + rank-level intervention 证明 + lattice-specific marginalization 解决检索身份翻转。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:112:- FlipReID（2105.05639）：ReID 常用 flip-mean TTA → **必须反复强调非 TTA 换名**（同 K vs-TTA 控制，lattice 多 +3.04）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:116:operating point 低（只在 h≤24 强 +3-4 mAP，h=32 marginal）→ 主动收窄定位 **"severe low-resolution / cross-resolution ReID under sampling-lattice uncertainty"**，不写成通用 ReID 鲁棒性稿。h=32 marginal 反而支持机制（分辨率升高 → lattice uncertainty 消退）。真正风险 = 训练版打不过 frozen ensemble，那就只是 ensemble trick。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:121:2. 实现 LM-ReID（插件式，config 开关，lattice aug 复用 `cvpb_lattice_killswitch.py` 的 LR 生成）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:125:6. eval（**test.py 不用 train.py**）：LM-ReID(K marg) vs single / TTA / frozen-ensemble，h=16/24/32 分报，LEG/LOTG/PRF。

./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:3018:I did not find an exact prior for “sampling-lattice hidden variable / sub-pixel phase marginalization” in low-res ReID. Closest works model resolution gap or resolution-invariant features, not lattice phase marginalization: VPFA, LRAR, RFD; BlurPool covers general anti-aliasing/shift-invariance but not ReID lattice marginalization. Sources: [VPFA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00936), [LRAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2207.13037), [RFD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2109.07871), [BlurPool](https://arxiv.org/abs/1904.11486).

./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1571:experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lattice_result.md:53:→ lattice 边缘化是**低分辨率现象**（h≤24 强、h=32 临界、h≥48 无），正是 d_8.txt 预测的 regime。**脚本自带 AUTO VERDICT: GO（h=16/24 过全部四关：volatility ✓ / 增益≥2 ✓ / LAT-TTA>0 ✓ / phase-var|#false>0 ✓）。**
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1572:experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lattice_result.md:59:**边缘化有效且明显超普通 TTA（GO 的承重证据）**：K=9 phase-MaxSim 边缘化把 16px mAP 从 42.6→46.9（**+4.23**），而**同 K 同 MaxSim 融合的普通 TTA 只 +1.19**，**LAT-TTA=+3.04**。MaxSim 取 K 中最优本可能仅靠"多枪"涨点，但 TTA-MaxSim 同样多枪只拿 +1.19——**差值证明增益来自 lattice 结构本身，不是 TTA 换名**。双跑一致（run1 +4.25 / LAT-TTA +3.04，run2 +4.23 / LAT-TTA +3.04）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1573:experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lattice_result.md:64:3. 操作点很低（16px Market query vs HR gallery，mAP 42→47）。+4.23 是真实相对改善（~10% rel），但绝对精度远未可用——符合 frozen 零训练探针，支持"去训练 lattice-consistency / phase-marginalized embedding"的下一步。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1574:experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lattice_result.md:71:- **variant0=确定性 bicubic LR**：lattice 与 TTA 共享 variant0，保证单图 baseline 一致、对照干净。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1575:experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lattice_result.md:72:- **MaxSim 对照陷阱**：必须用 TTA-MaxSim（同 K 同 max）当基线，否则把"多枪"误当 lattice 增益（OVLI 教训：我们提的 MaxSim 不能输给 trivial 基线——这里 lattice-MaxSim 46.9 > TTA-MaxSim 43.8 > 单图 42.6，干净）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1594:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview/reviews/lit_4.md:555:- A 语义/VLM teacher: 已完成,见 experiments/paper_materials/newinfo_semantic_teacher-codex.md。结论是遮挡语义 teacher 对 SOLIDER 强主干整体偏负,FLaN-Net/π-VL/AG-ReID/LVLM-ReID/RMPSNet 等发表遮挡成绩多低于或接近现有强基线; 唯一值得先探的是属性 teacher 的可靠性噪声屏蔽,其次是 teacher 只用于属性级样本组织,两者都必须先做零训练探针。

./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1616:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview/reviews/lit_19.md:2668:和这批最像工作的区别：它最像 HDNet 的“层级解耦”，但 HDNet 用对抗学习去掉相机和模态噪声，我们用 SMPL 投影显式定义哪些身体表面在航拍和地面视角中可比。它也不同于 LVLM-ReID 的语义 token，我们的 token 是几何可见性和人体表面，不是语言生成语义。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1617:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview/reviews/lit_19.md:2698:和这批最像工作的区别：它最像 LVLM-ReID，但 LVLM-ReID 推理时仍用 LVLM 生成 `<REID>` token，成本高且语义偏外观。我们的 token 来自 SMPL joints、mesh visibility、2D projection 和 pose confidence，只在训练期蒸馏到 SOLIDER 特征，测试仍是单 embedding。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1618:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview/reviews/lit_19.md:2744:和这批最像工作的区别：它最像 HDNet 的“层级解耦”，但 HDNet 用对抗学习去掉相机和模态噪声，我们用 SMPL 投影显式定义哪些身体表面在航拍和地面视角中可比。它也不同于 LVLM-ReID 的语义 token，我们的 token 是几何可见性和人体表面，不是语言生成语义。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1619:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview/reviews/lit_19.md:2774:和这批最像工作的区别：它最像 LVLM-ReID，但 LVLM-ReID 推理时仍用 LVLM 生成 `<REID>` token，成本高且语义偏外观。我们的 token 来自 SMPL joints、mesh visibility、2D projection 和 pose confidence，只在训练期蒸馏到 SOLIDER 特征，测试仍是单 embedding。

./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1669:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/reviews/deep_28.md:6930:它把 LVLM 从“生成描述的外部工具”改写成“产生身份语义 token 的特征提取器组成部分”。关键词是 LVLM-ReID、Pedestrian Semantic Token Generation、`<REID>` token、Semantic-Guided Interaction、reinforced semantic token、generative language models assist discriminative visual models。旧问题是“怎么给 ReID 加文本语义”，新问题是“怎样把 LVLM 的生成过程变成 ReID 的身份表示”。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1670:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/reviews/deep_28.md:7020:它把 LVLM 从“生成描述的外部工具”改写成“产生身份语义 token 的特征提取器组成部分”。关键词是 LVLM-ReID、Pedestrian Semantic Token Generation、`<REID>` token、Semantic-Guided Interaction、reinforced semantic token、generative language models assist discriminative visual models。旧问题是“怎么给 ReID 加文本语义”，新问题是“怎样把 LVLM 的生成过程变成 ReID 的身份表示”。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1735:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview/reviews/lit_2.md:322:- A 语义/VLM teacher: 已完成,见 experiments/paper_materials/newinfo_semantic_teacher-codex.md。结论是遮挡语义 teacher 对 SOLIDER 强主干整体偏负,FLaN-Net/π-VL/AG-ReID/LVLM-ReID/RMPSNet 等发表遮挡成绩多低于或接近现有强基线; 唯一值得先探的是属性 teacher 的可靠性噪声屏蔽,其次是 teacher 只用于属性级样本组织,两者都必须先做零训练探针。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1736:experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lattice_killswitch_DESIGN.md:3:> 脚本: `cvpb_lattice_killswitch.py`  机会来源: `litreview2/explore20/clean/d_8.txt` 机会1（信心 7/10）
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1737:experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lattice_killswitch_DESIGN.md:7:低分辨率 ReID 失败**不是**"模糊缺细节"，而是**采样格点不确定性**：一个人在 h=16-32px 时 = 一族 alias/crop-lattice 观测（亚像素采样相位 / ±1 LR-pixel bbox 量化 / antialias kernel / 轻微 detector crop error）。模型只见过其中一个。身份匹配应对这族观测**边缘化**，而不是把某个确定性 LR 图当真值。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1738:experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lattice_killswitch_DESIGN.md:13:- **K=9 lattice 变体**（每 LR query，从同一 HR 图）：variant0 = 确定性 bicubic LR（= 单图 baseline）；其余轮转覆盖三轴：亚像素相位平移（±0.5 LR 像素的 HR 仿射）、±1 LR-pixel bbox crop shift、bbox expand/contract 1 LR 像素 + kernel 切换（bicubic/bilinear/lanczos/box/hamming）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1739:experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lattice_killswitch_DESIGN.md:17:1. **vs 普通 TTA**：相同 K、相同融合（mean / MaxSim），但 K 个 view 是普通 test-time aug（pad+RandomCrop + hflip）of 单一 bicubic LR，**无 lattice 语义**。phase-lattice 必须明显超过普通 TTA（否则只是 TTA 换名）。MaxSim 对照尤其关键：MaxSim 取 K 中最优可能仅靠"多枪"涨点，TTA-MaxSim 同样多枪 → 差值才是 lattice 特有增益。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1740:experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lattice_killswitch_DESIGN.md:27:- K 选 9：覆盖三轴 + variant0 = 单图 baseline（lattice 与 TTA 共享 variant0，保证 baseline 一致）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1742:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/reviews/deep_9.md:823:- A 语义/VLM teacher: 已完成,见 experiments/paper_materials/newinfo_semantic_teacher-codex.md。结论是遮挡语义 teacher 对 SOLIDER 强主干整体偏负,FLaN-Net/π-VL/AG-ReID/LVLM-ReID/RMPSNet 等发表遮挡成绩多低于或接近现有强基线; 唯一值得先探的是属性 teacher 的可靠性噪声屏蔽,其次是 teacher 只用于属性级样本组织,两者都必须先做零训练探针。

./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1906:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:3:GO 可以继续，但要把稿子定位成 **severe LR / cross-resolution ReID 方法稿**，不要写成通用 ReID 鲁棒性稿。当前证据最强的是 intervention：固定模型、固定身份、只改变 LR 采样格点，rank-1 身份大规模翻转，且 lattice-K 明显强于同 K 普通 TTA。这个足够支撑一个新问题定义，但方法稿必须拿出训练版，否则容易被审稿人打成“强 TTA/ensemble trick”。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1907:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:6:如果训练版 `LM-ReID + K marginalization` 能在 h=16 上比 frozen lattice ensemble 再高 **+0.8 到 +2.0 mAP**，并且 h=24 仍有稳定收益，信心到 **8/10**。如果只停留在零训练 ensemble，最多 **5/10**。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1908:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:10:名字建议：**Lattice-Marginalized ReID, LM-ReID**。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1909:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:43:L_adv = GRL-CE(Dφ(z_i^l), lattice_label_l)
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1910:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:57:`L_adv` 不要当主贡献，作为辅助去掉 embedding 中可预测的 lattice label。主贡献是 hidden lattice marginalization。adversarial 太强可能擦掉身份边缘细节，所以必须有 ablation：`marg only / marg+cons / marg+cons+adv`。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1911:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:62:2. fine-tune 阶段从 HR train image 在线生成 LR lattice variants。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1912:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:63:3. 每张图每 iter 采样 `M=2-4` 个 lattice variants，周期性或 eval 用完整 `K=9`。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1913:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:73:frozen lattice-K ensemble +0.8 到 +2.0 mAP
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1914:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:80:允许 marginal，但不能负；至少说明 lattice uncertainty 已下降
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1915:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:93:5. lattice variants 固定 K=9，主实验用 3x3 phase；bbox jitter/kernel variants 做 ablation，不要无限扩 K。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1916:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:100:必须新增 lattice uncertainty 报告：
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1917:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:110:LEG = mAP(lattice marginal K) - mAP(canonical single)
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1918:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:113:LOTG = mAP(lattice K) - mAP(random TTA K)
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1919:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:116:ΔAP_i = AP_i(lattice K) - AP_i(single)
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1920:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:132:2. **vs-#false**：控制“多试几次总会撞上更好结果”的假象，可以用 shuffled lattice labels、非 lattice photometric variants、或 same-K random harmless variants。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1921:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:136:VPFA 是最近最接近的强邻居。它把 CR-ReID 看成 feature space 中的 resolution direction，并用 Vector Panning 模块把 LR feature 映射到 pseudo-HR feature；论文还强调 SR 和 resolution-invariant 方法的瓶颈。([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00936)) 但它的 hidden variable 是“resolution gap / feature direction”，不是 sub-pixel sampling lattice，也不是对 lattice variants 做 marginalization。边界清楚，但必须正面对比。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1922:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:138:LRAR 也是强相关旧邻居。它做 resolution-adaptive representation，用动态度量、varying-length representation 和 resolution-adaptive masks 处理 HR-LR 比较。([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/2207.13037)) 它占了“resolution adaptive”这个词，不能用这个当 novelty。你的 novelty 应写成 sampling-lattice uncertainty。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1923:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:140:RFD 处理 multi-resolution gallery，用 feature distillation/filtering 去掉 resolution-related feature。([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/2109.07871)) 它占了“resolution-invariant distillation”，但没有占 lattice intervention。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1924:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:144:ITKM 是 2026 unsupervised multi-scenario ReID，用 CLIP/image-text knowledge modeling，把 cross-resolution、换衣、跨模态等放进统一无监督框架。([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.11243)) 它不是 lattice 机制，但如果你做 unsupervised 设置会撞；建议主稿先做 supervised CR-ReID。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1925:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:146:通用 anti-aliasing/shift-invariance 已经很老，BlurPool 明确指出小输入平移会导致 CNN 输出大变，downsampling 忽略 sampling theorem，低通 anti-alias 可改善鲁棒性。([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/1904.11486?utm_source=openai)) 所以不能声称“首次发现 aliasing/phase 影响深度模型”。能声称的是：**首次在 LR person ReID 中把采样格点作为隐藏变量，用 rank-level intervention 证明其影响，并用 lattice-specific marginalization 解决检索身份翻转。**
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1926:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:148:ReID 里的 TTA 也不能忽略。FlipReID 已指出 ReID 常用 original+horizontal flip 的 feature mean，并专门处理训练-推理 TTA gap。([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/2105.05639?utm_source=openai)) 所以你必须反复强调：这不是普通 TTA 换名，因为你有同 K vs-TTA 控制，且 lattice-K 比 TTA 多 +3.04 mAP。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1927:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:169:2. 这个因素通过 intervention 证明：只改 lattice，不改身份、场景、模型，rank-1 身份大量翻转。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1928:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:170:3. 普通 TTA 控制说明收益不是“多看几次”的泛化收益，而是 lattice-specific。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1929:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:171:4. phase-var 与 h 共线不否定机制，说明 lattice uncertainty 主要在 severe LR 条件下被激活。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1930:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:172:5. 因此方法不是预测哪张 query 会失败，而是在所有 severe LR query 上对隐藏 lattice 做 marginalization。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1931:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:179:severe low-resolution / cross-resolution ReID under sampling-lattice uncertainty
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1932:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:182:h=32 marginal 不是致命问题，反而支持机制：分辨率升高后 lattice uncertainty 消退。真正风险是训练版如果只比普通 TTA 高、但打不过 frozen lattice ensemble，就不像方法；如果能超过 frozen ensemble或显著降 K，故事就成立。

./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1968:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/ondisk_pivot.md:2150:./reviews/deep_13.md:662:- A 语义/VLM teacher: 已完成,见 experiments/paper_materials/newinfo_semantic_teacher-codex.md。结论是遮挡语义 teacher 对 SOLIDER 强主干整体偏负,FLaN-Net/π-VL/AG-ReID/LVLM-ReID/RMPSNet 等发表遮挡成绩多低于或接近现有强基线; 唯一值得先探的是属性 teacher 的可靠性噪声屏蔽,其次是 teacher 只用于属性级样本组织,两者都必须先做零训练探针。

./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:2357:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/ondisk_pivot.md:4824:./reassess/r_3.md:9614:analyses/deep_28.txt:77:它把 LVLM 从“生成描述的外部工具”改写成“产生身份语义 token 的特征提取器组成部分”。关键词是 LVLM-ReID、Pedestrian Semantic Token Generation、`<REID>` token、Semantic-Guided Interaction、reinforced semantic token、generative language models assist discriminative visual models。旧问题是“怎么给 ReID 加文本语义”，新问题是“怎样把 LVLM 的生成过程变成 ReID 的身份表示”。

./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:2574:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/reviews/deep_27.md:504:- A 语义/VLM teacher: 已完成,见 experiments/paper_materials/newinfo_semantic_teacher-codex.md。结论是遮挡语义 teacher 对 SOLIDER 强主干整体偏负,FLaN-Net/π-VL/AG-ReID/LVLM-ReID/RMPSNet 等发表遮挡成绩多低于或接近现有强基线; 唯一值得先探的是属性 teacher 的可靠性噪声屏蔽,其次是 teacher 只用于属性级样本组织,两者都必须先做零训练探针。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:2973:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/reassess/r_2.md:1814:reassess/r_3.md:4009:/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview/reviews/lit_19.md:2668:和这批最像工作的区别：它最像 HDNet 的“层级解耦”，但 HDNet 用对抗学习去掉相机和模态噪声，我们用 SMPL 投影显式定义哪些身体表面在航拍和地面视角中可比。它也不同于 LVLM-ReID 的语义 token，我们的 token 是几何可见性和人体表面，不是语言生成语义。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:2979:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/reassess/r_2.md:1820:reassess/r_3.md:4016:/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview/reviews/lit_19.md:2698:和这批最像工作的区别：它最像 LVLM-ReID，但 LVLM-ReID 推理时仍用 LVLM 生成 `<REID>` token，成本高且语义偏外观。我们的 token 来自 SMPL joints、mesh visibility、2D projection 和 pose confidence，只在训练期蒸馏到 SOLIDER 特征，测试仍是单 embedding。

./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3824:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/analyses/deep_28.txt:77:它把 LVLM 从“生成描述的外部工具”改写成“产生身份语义 token 的特征提取器组成部分”。关键词是 LVLM-ReID、Pedestrian Semantic Token Generation、`<REID>` token、Semantic-Guided Interaction、reinforced semantic token、generative language models assist discriminative visual models。旧问题是“怎么给 ReID 加文本语义”，新问题是“怎样把 LVLM 的生成过程变成 ReID 的身份表示”。

./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3866:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:1:# 实验 exp359: LM-ReID (Lattice-Marginalized ReID)
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3867:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:3:> 后 PRCV「换问题」阶段第一个 GO 的 method 候选（9 个零训练 cheap-kill 全死后）。零训练 kill-switch 已 GO（d8 lattice, agent a68e6），method-design codex 判 **CCF-B 7/10**（训练版超 frozen ensemble → 8/10）。本实验做训练版，证明 lattice-marginalization 是 **method 不是 ensemble trick**。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3868:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:4:> 设计来源：`experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt`（完整 codex 设计）+ `cvpb_lattice_result.md`（kill-switch 数据）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3869:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:8:低分辨率 ReID 的传统视角：LR = 模糊/缺细节，解法 = SR / resolution-invariant feature。**我们重定义**：一部分 LR 失败不是"缺信息"，而是 **采样格点不确定性（sampling-lattice uncertainty）**——同一个 HR 身份在不同合法的 LR 采样格点（sub-pixel phase / bbox alignment / downsample kernel）下，落到不同 embedding 区域，导致 rank-1 身份翻转。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3870:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:11:frozen exp260b Market，K=9 lattice variants ensemble，HR gallery / LR query：
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3871:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:20:两个生死控制都过：**vs 普通 TTA**（同 K 同 MaxSim，lattice 比 TTA 多 +3.04，证 lattice-specific 非 TTA 换名；lat-MaxSim 46.9 > tta 43.8 > single 42.6，无"输给 trivial baseline"问题）；**vs #false-in-topk**（phase-var partial +0.19/+0.21 存活）。机制：phase-var 是 TTA-var 的 **7.5×**；74.9% h=16 query 跨 lattice 翻转 rank-1，随 h 升单调衰减到 1.2%（h=48 机制消失 = 低分辨率专用现象）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3872:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:23:phase-var 作 per-query 失败预测器**不干净**（控 LR-severity 后 partial 塌到 ≈0，与 per-image LR 失真共线）。**GO 靠的是 interventional 结果**（ensemble +4.2 / LAT−TTA +3.0 是直接测量）。故事写成 "lattice sensitivity 是 **mechanism-level nuisance**, 不是 standalone failure predictor"，方法是对所有 severe-LR query 做 marginalization（不是预测哪张失败）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3873:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:27:训练一个 lattice-marginalized embedding（对 lattice variants 身份稳定）+ 推理 K-marginalization，在 h=16 上比 frozen lattice ensemble **再高 +0.8~2.0 mAP** → 证明它学到了 lattice-invariance（是 method），不是 ensemble trick。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3874:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:33:2. fine-tune：HR train image 在线生成 LR lattice variants `x^l = U(D_l(x))`，l ∈ {sub-pixel phase, bbox jitter, downsample kernel}。每图每 iter 采样 M=2-4 variants，eval 用 K=9。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3875:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:36:### LM-ReID loss
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3876:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:42:L_adv  = GRL-CE(Dφ(z^l), lattice_label_l)                        # 弱：去掉 embedding 中可预测 lattice label
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3877:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:60:失败最可能原因：训练版只 ≈ frozen ensemble（没学到额外 lattice-invariance）→ 沦为 test-time ensemble trick，不成方法稿。备选投稿角度：同等 mAP 下 K 从 9 降到 3 或 single inference 保留大部分收益。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3878:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:66:- **frozen lattice ensemble**（零训练 K=9，= kill-switch 的 +4.23，这是训练版必须超过的硬线）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3879:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:75:- 新指标：PRF@1（phase rank-flip rate）、Flip Entropy、LEG（lattice ensemble gain）、LOTG（lattice-over-TTA gain）、query ΔAP。按 h 分报 + paired bootstrap 95% CI + K=1/3/5/9 曲线 + compute cost。PRF 随 h（74.9%→31%→10%→1.2%）是强故事线。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3880:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:79:- **VPFA**（2510.00936，最近邻）：CR-ReID = feature-space resolution direction，Vector Panning LR→pseudo-HR。hidden variable 是 resolution gap/feature direction，**不是 sampling lattice，不做 lattice marginalization**。必须正面对比。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3881:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:80:- LRAR（2207.13037）：resolution-adaptive representation，占"resolution adaptive"词 → 我们 novelty 写 sampling-lattice uncertainty。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3882:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:81:- RFD（2109.07871）：multi-res gallery distillation，占"resolution-invariant distillation"，没占 lattice intervention。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3883:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:82:- BlurPool（1904.11486）：anti-aliasing/shift-invariance 已老 → **不能说首次发现 aliasing 影响**。**能声称**：首次在 LR person ReID 把采样格点作隐藏变量 + rank-level intervention 证明 + lattice-specific marginalization 解决检索身份翻转。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3884:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:83:- FlipReID（2105.05639）：ReID 常用 flip-mean TTA → **必须反复强调非 TTA 换名**（同 K vs-TTA 控制，lattice 多 +3.04）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3885:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:87:operating point 低（只在 h≤24 强 +3-4 mAP，h=32 marginal）→ 主动收窄定位 **"severe low-resolution / cross-resolution ReID under sampling-lattice uncertainty"**，不写成通用 ReID 鲁棒性稿。h=32 marginal 反而支持机制（分辨率升高 → lattice uncertainty 消退）。真正风险 = 训练版打不过 frozen ensemble，那就只是 ensemble trick。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3886:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:92:2. 实现 LM-ReID（插件式，config 开关，lattice aug 复用 `cvpb_lattice_killswitch.py` 的 LR 生成）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3887:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:96:6. eval（**test.py 不用 train.py**）：LM-ReID(K marg) vs single / TTA / frozen-ensemble，h=16/24/32 分报，LEG/LOTG/PRF。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3888:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/claude_review.md:1:# Claude Broad Review — exp359 LM-ReID 训练脚本
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3890:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/claude_review.md:8:- **不是小调参**：LM-ReID 是新机制（lattice-marginalized embedding），重定义低分辨率 ReID = 采样格点不确定性；有零训练 kill-switch GO（独立轴、双 trivial 控制 vs-TTA/vs-#false 都过、GO 靠 interventional 直接测量）做前置，codex 判 CCF-B 7/10。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3891:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/claude_review.md:9:- **单变量**：唯一变量 = "训练 lattice-invariance" vs "frozen 模型"。eval 复用 kill-switch（byte-identical degradation），与 frozen baseline 只差"是否 fine-tune"。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3892:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/claude_review.md:23:- **L_adv** = GRL：disc 预测 variant slot，梯度反转使 z 不含可预测 lattice label。`slot=arange(M).repeat(B)` 与 image-major reshape 对齐。warmup-gated（adv_start），默认 lam_adv=0（弱辅助先不开，可后续 ablation）。✓
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3893:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/claude_review.md:29:- lattice 生成函数（make_lr/make_lattice_variants/pil_to_tensor_np/_to_target_aspect）**逐行复制自 cvpb_lattice_killswitch.py** → 训练降质与 GO kill-switch eval **byte-identical**（单变量隔离的关键）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3894:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/claude_review.md:43:逐行审查 + 源码接口验证完成，4 处问题已修（KL 方向 / AMP / 稳健 out 取值 / scaler backward）。代码正确、对照隔离干净（lattice 生成 byte-identical 复用、eval 复用 kill-switch）、过线判据明确、诚实判死路径清楚。**审查通过**，可进 smoke + 训练（仍需 Codex 独立审通过）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3896:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_final.txt:8:- **High** [cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:173](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:173): per-sample RNG is reset from `seed + idx`, so each image gets the same height and same lattice variants every epoch. This contradicts design “每图每 iter 采样 M variants”.
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3897:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_final.txt:11:- **High** [cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:48](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:48), [cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:118](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:118): default `M=2` plus `mode = j % 3` trains only canonical + bbox-shift/bilinear. It does not train sub-pixel phase or zoom/antialias axes despite the method claim.
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3898:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_final.txt:12:  Fix: make real training default `M>=4` at minimum, or sample perturbation mode/kernel for each noncanonical variant; if keeping `L_adv`, store actual lattice labels.
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3900:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_final.txt:17:- **Medium** [cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:345](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:345): `L_id` triplet is computed over flattened `B*M`, not `mean_l Triplet(gf[:, l], y)`. This treats other variants of the same image as positives and is not exactly the stated per-lattice-slot formula.
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3901:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_final.txt:20:- **Low** [cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:96](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:96): lattice generation appears output-equivalent to kill-switch, but not literally byte-identical source.  
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3902:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_final.txt:34:I did not find an exact prior for “sampling-lattice hidden variable / sub-pixel phase marginalization” in low-res ReID. Closest works model resolution gap or resolution-invariant features, not lattice phase marginalization: VPFA, LRAR, RFD; BlurPool covers general anti-aliasing/shift-invariance but not ReID lattice marginalization. Sources: [VPFA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00936), [LRAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2207.13037), [RFD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2109.07871), [BlurPool](https://arxiv.org/abs/1904.11486).
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3903:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:14:Review the LM-ReID fine-tuning script: the single file matching experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lm_reid_*.py (exp359; design at experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md). 它 fine-tune frozen SOLIDER PoseBackboneModel(pose_dict=None → 纯 backbone global feat)成 lattice-marginalized embedding 抗采样格点(sub-pixel phase / +-1 LR-px bbox / antialias kernel)。请先 cat 该文件 + model/pose_backbone_model.py 的 forward。逐行审:

./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3905:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:24:也用 web search 查 lattice-marginalization / sub-pixel sampling uncertainty for low-res ReID 是否有 exact 先例。输出: Verdict (approve / needs-attention) + findings (severity Critical/High/Medium/Low, 每条带行号+修法).
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3908:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:30:# 实验 exp359: LM-ReID (Lattice-Marginalized ReID)
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3909:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:32:> 后 PRCV「换问题」阶段第一个 GO 的 method 候选（9 个零训练 cheap-kill 全死后）。零训练 kill-switch 已 GO（d8 lattice, agent a68e6），method-design codex 判 **CCF-B 7/10**（训练版超 frozen ensemble → 8/10）。本实验做训练版，证明 lattice-marginalization 是 **method 不是 ensemble trick**。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3910:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:33:> 设计来源：`experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt`（完整 codex 设计）+ `cvpb_lattice_result.md`（kill-switch 数据）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3911:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:37:低分辨率 ReID 的传统视角：LR = 模糊/缺细节，解法 = SR / resolution-invariant feature。**我们重定义**：一部分 LR 失败不是"缺信息"，而是 **采样格点不确定性（sampling-lattice uncertainty）**——同一个 HR 身份在不同合法的 LR 采样格点（sub-pixel phase / bbox alignment / downsample kernel）下，落到不同 embedding 区域，导致 rank-1 身份翻转。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3912:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:40:frozen exp260b Market，K=9 lattice variants ensemble，HR gallery / LR query：
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3913:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:49:两个生死控制都过：**vs 普通 TTA**（同 K 同 MaxSim，lattice 比 TTA 多 +3.04，证 lattice-specific 非 TTA 换名；lat-MaxSim 46.9 > tta 43.8 > single 42.6，无"输给 trivial baseline"问题）；**vs #false-in-topk**（phase-var partial +0.19/+0.21 存活）。机制：phase-var 是 TTA-var 的 **7.5×**；74.9% h=16 query 跨 lattice 翻转 rank-1，随 h 升单调衰减到 1.2%（h=48 机制消失 = 低分辨率专用现象）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3914:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:52:phase-var 作 per-query 失败预测器**不干净**（控 LR-severity 后 partial 塌到 ≈0，与 per-image LR 失真共线）。**GO 靠的是 interventional 结果**（ensemble +4.2 / LAT−TTA +3.0 是直接测量）。故事写成 "lattice sensitivity 是 **mechanism-level nuisance**, 不是 standalone failure predictor"，方法是对所有 severe-LR query 做 marginalization（不是预测哪张失败）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3915:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:56:训练一个 lattice-marginalized embedding（对 lattice variants 身份稳定）+ 推理 K-marginalization，在 h=16 上比 frozen lattice ensemble **再高 +0.8~2.0 mAP** → 证明它学到了 lattice-invariance（是 method），不是 ensemble trick。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3916:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:62:2. fine-tune：HR train image 在线生成 LR lattice variants `x^l = U(D_l(x))`，l ∈ {sub-pixel phase, bbox jitter, downsample kernel}。每图每 iter 采样 M=2-4 variants，eval 用 K=9。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3917:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:65:### LM-ReID loss
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3918:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:71:L_adv  = GRL-CE(Dφ(z^l), lattice_label_l)                        # 弱：去掉 embedding 中可预测 lattice label
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3919:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:89:失败最可能原因：训练版只 ≈ frozen ensemble（没学到额外 lattice-invariance）→ 沦为 test-time ensemble trick，不成方法稿。备选投稿角度：同等 mAP 下 K 从 9 降到 3 或 single inference 保留大部分收益。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3920:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:95:- **frozen lattice ensemble**（零训练 K=9，= kill-switch 的 +4.23，这是训练版必须超过的硬线）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3921:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:104:- 新指标：PRF@1（phase rank-flip rate）、Flip Entropy、LEG（lattice ensemble gain）、LOTG（lattice-over-TTA gain）、query ΔAP。按 h 分报 + paired bootstrap 95% CI + K=1/3/5/9 曲线 + compute cost。PRF 随 h（74.9%→31%→10%→1.2%）是强故事线。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3922:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:108:- **VPFA**（2510.00936，最近邻）：CR-ReID = feature-space resolution direction，Vector Panning LR→pseudo-HR。hidden variable 是 resolution gap/feature direction，**不是 sampling lattice，不做 lattice marginalization**。必须正面对比。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3923:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:109:- LRAR（2207.13037）：resolution-adaptive representation，占"resolution adaptive"词 → 我们 novelty 写 sampling-lattice uncertainty。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3924:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:110:- RFD（2109.07871）：multi-res gallery distillation，占"resolution-invariant distillation"，没占 lattice intervention。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3925:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:111:- BlurPool（1904.11486）：anti-aliasing/shift-invariance 已老 → **不能说首次发现 aliasing 影响**。**能声称**：首次在 LR person ReID 把采样格点作隐藏变量 + rank-level intervention 证明 + lattice-specific marginalization 解决检索身份翻转。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3926:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:112:- FlipReID（2105.05639）：ReID 常用 flip-mean TTA → **必须反复强调非 TTA 换名**（同 K vs-TTA 控制，lattice 多 +3.04）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3927:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:116:operating point 低（只在 h≤24 强 +3-4 mAP，h=32 marginal）→ 主动收窄定位 **"severe low-resolution / cross-resolution ReID under sampling-lattice uncertainty"**，不写成通用 ReID 鲁棒性稿。h=32 marginal 反而支持机制（分辨率升高 → lattice uncertainty 消退）。真正风险 = 训练版打不过 frozen ensemble，那就只是 ensemble trick。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3928:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:121:2. 实现 LM-ReID（插件式，config 开关，lattice aug 复用 `cvpb_lattice_killswitch.py` 的 LR 生成）。

./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4037:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw.md:3018:I did not find an exact prior for “sampling-lattice hidden variable / sub-pixel phase marginalization” in low-res ReID. This contradicts design “每图每 iter 采样 M variants”.

./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4052:# 实验 exp359: LM-ReID (Lattice-Marginalized ReID)
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4054:> 后 PRCV「换问题」阶段第一个 GO 的 method 候选（9 个零训练 cheap-kill 全死后）。零训练 kill-switch 已 GO（d8 lattice, agent a68e6），method-design codex 判 **CCF-B 7/10**（训练版超 frozen ensemble → 8/10）。本实验做训练版，证明 lattice-marginalization 是 **method 不是 ensemble trick**。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4055:> 设计来源：`experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt`（完整 codex 设计）+ `cvpb_lattice_result.md`（kill-switch 数据）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4059:低分辨率 ReID 的传统视角：LR = 模糊/缺细节，解法 = SR / resolution-invariant feature。**我们重定义**：一部分 LR 失败不是"缺信息"，而是 **采样格点不确定性（sampling-lattice uncertainty）**——同一个 HR 身份在不同合法的 LR 采样格点（sub-pixel phase / bbox alignment / downsample kernel）下，落到不同 embedding 区域，导致 rank-1 身份翻转。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4062:frozen exp260b Market，K=9 lattice variants ensemble，HR gallery / LR query：
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4071:两个生死控制都过：**vs 普通 TTA**（同 K 同 MaxSim，lattice 比 TTA 多 +3.04，证 lattice-specific 非 TTA 换名；lat-MaxSim 46.9 > tta 43.8 > single 42.6，无"输给 trivial baseline"问题）；**vs #false-in-topk**（phase-var partial +0.19/+0.21 存活）。机制：phase-var 是 TTA-var 的 **7.5×**；74.9% h=16 query 跨 lattice 翻转 rank-1，随 h 升单调衰减到 1.2%（h=48 机制消失 = 低分辨率专用现象）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4074:phase-var 作 per-query 失败预测器**不干净**（控 LR-severity 后 partial 塌到 ≈0，与 per-image LR 失真共线）。**GO 靠的是 interventional 结果**（ensemble +4.2 / LAT−TTA +3.0 是直接测量）。故事写成 "lattice sensitivity 是 **mechanism-level nuisance**, 不是 standalone failure predictor"，方法是对所有 severe-LR query 做 marginalization（不是预测哪张失败）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4078:训练一个 lattice-marginalized embedding（对 lattice variants 身份稳定）+ 推理 K-marginalization，在 h=16 上比 frozen lattice ensemble **再高 +0.8~2.0 mAP** → 证明它学到了 lattice-invariance（是 method），不是 ensemble trick。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4084:2. fine-tune：HR train image 在线生成 LR lattice variants `x^l = U(D_l(x))`，l ∈ {sub-pixel phase, bbox jitter, downsample kernel}。每图每 iter 采样 M=2-4 variants，eval 用 K=9。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4087:### LM-ReID loss
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4091:L_marg = -log[ mean_l p^l[y] ] + Triplet(z^μ, y)                 # marginal likelihood（主贡献）
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4093:L_adv  = GRL-CE(Dφ(z^l), lattice_label_l)                        # 弱：去掉 embedding 中可预测 lattice label
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4098:### 推理 K-marginalization
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4109:- h=32：允许 marginal 不负。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4111:失败最可能原因：训练版只 ≈ frozen ensemble（没学到额外 lattice-invariance）→ 沦为 test-time ensemble trick，不成方法稿。备选投稿角度：同等 mAP 下 K 从 9 降到 3 或 single inference 保留大部分收益。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4117:- **frozen lattice ensemble**（零训练 K=9，= kill-switch 的 +4.23，这是训练版必须超过的硬线）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4120:消融：marg only / marg+cons / marg+cons+adv；τ sweep；K=1/3/5/9 曲线；phase-only vs +bbox+kernel。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4124:- 合成：Market/MSMT，gallery HR，query LR h=16/24/32，canonical LR single baseline，K=9（3×3 phase 主，bbox/kernel ablation，不无限扩 K）。所有 TTA 对照 **K-matched**。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4126:- 新指标：PRF@1（phase rank-flip rate）、Flip Entropy、LEG（lattice ensemble gain）、LOTG（lattice-over-TTA gain）、query ΔAP。按 h 分报 + paired bootstrap 95% CI + K=1/3/5/9 曲线 + compute cost。PRF 随 h（74.9%→31%→10%→1.2%）是强故事线。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4130:- **VPFA**（2510.00936，最近邻）：CR-ReID = feature-space resolution direction，Vector Panning LR→pseudo-HR。hidden variable 是 resolution gap/feature direction，**不是 sampling lattice，不做 lattice marginalization**。必须正面对比。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4131:- LRAR（2207.13037）：resolution-adaptive representation，占"resolution adaptive"词 → 我们 novelty 写 sampling-lattice uncertainty。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4132:- RFD（2109.07871）：multi-res gallery distillation，占"resolution-invariant distillation"，没占 lattice intervention。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4133:- BlurPool（1904.11486）：anti-aliasing/shift-invariance 已老 → **不能说首次发现 aliasing 影响**。**能声称**：首次在 LR person ReID 把采样格点作隐藏变量 + rank-level intervention 证明 + lattice-specific marginalization 解决检索身份翻转。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4134:- FlipReID（2105.05639）：ReID 常用 flip-mean TTA → **必须反复强调非 TTA 换名**（同 K vs-TTA 控制，lattice 多 +3.04）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4138:operating point 低（只在 h≤24 强 +3-4 mAP，h=32 marginal）→ 主动收窄定位 **"severe low-resolution / cross-resolution ReID under sampling-lattice uncertainty"**，不写成通用 ReID 鲁棒性稿。h=32 marginal 反而支持机制（分辨率升高 → lattice uncertainty 消退）。真正风险 = 训练版打不过 frozen ensemble，那就只是 ensemble trick。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4143:2. 实现 LM-ReID（插件式，config 开关，lattice aug 复用 `cvpb_lattice_killswitch.py` 的 LR 生成）。

./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4483:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1738:experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lattice_killswitch_DESIGN.md:13:- **K=9 lattice 变体**（每 LR query，从同一 HR 图）：variant0 = 确定性 bicubic LR（= 单图 baseline）；其余轮转覆盖三轴：亚像素相位平移（±0.5 LR 像素的 HR 仿射）、±1 LR-pixel bbox crop shift、bbox expand/contract 1 LR 像素 + kernel 切换（bicubic/bilinear/lanczos/box/hamming）。

./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4490:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1911:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:62:2. fine-tune 阶段从 HR train image 在线生成 LR lattice variants。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4491:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1912:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:63:3. 每张图每 iter 采样 `M=2-4` 个 lattice variants，周期性或 eval 用完整 `K=9`。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4492:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1915:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:93:5. lattice variants 固定 K=9，主实验用 3x3 phase；bbox jitter/kernel variants 做 ablation，不要无限扩 K。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4493:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1920:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:132:2. **vs-#false**：控制“多试几次总会撞上更好结果”的假象，可以用 shuffled lattice labels、非 lattice photometric variants、或 same-K random harmless variants。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4494:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1921:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:136:VPFA 是最近最接近的强邻居。它把 CR-ReID 看成 feature space 中的 resolution direction，并用 Vector Panning 模块把 LR feature 映射到 pseudo-HR feature；论文还强调 SR 和 resolution-invariant 方法的瓶颈。([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00936)) 但它的 hidden variable 是“resolution gap / feature direction”，不是 sub-pixel sampling lattice，也不是对 lattice variants 做 marginalization。边界清楚，但必须正面对比。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4495:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1923:experiments/cargo_cvpb/litreview2/pivot/clean/lattice_method_design.txt:140:RFD 处理 multi-resolution gallery，用 feature distillation/filtering 去掉 resolution-related feature。([arxiv.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/2109.07871)) 它占了“resolution-invariant distillation”，但没有占 lattice intervention。

./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4593:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3864:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:1239:   391	                axis_lbl = axb.to(x.device).reshape(B * M)             # [B*M] real lattice axis
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4594:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3869:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:8:低分辨率 ReID 的传统视角：LR = 模糊/缺细节，解法 = SR / resolution-invariant feature。**我们重定义**：一部分 LR 失败不是"缺信息"，而是 **采样格点不确定性（sampling-lattice uncertainty）**——同一个 HR 身份在不同合法的 LR 采样格点（sub-pixel phase / bbox alignment / downsample kernel）下，落到不同 embedding 区域，导致 rank-1 身份翻转。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4595:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3870:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:11:frozen exp260b Market，K=9 lattice variants ensemble，HR gallery / LR query：
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4596:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3873:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:27:训练一个 lattice-marginalized embedding（对 lattice variants 身份稳定）+ 推理 K-marginalization，在 h=16 上比 frozen lattice ensemble **再高 +0.8~2.0 mAP** → 证明它学到了 lattice-invariance（是 method），不是 ensemble trick。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4597:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3874:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:33:2. fine-tune：HR train image 在线生成 LR lattice variants `x^l = U(D_l(x))`，l ∈ {sub-pixel phase, bbox jitter, downsample kernel}。每图每 iter 采样 M=2-4 variants，eval 用 K=9。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4598:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3876:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:42:L_adv  = GRL-CE(Dφ(z^l), lattice_label_l)                        # 弱：去掉 embedding 中可预测 lattice label
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4599:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3882:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design.md:81:- RFD（2109.07871）：multi-res gallery distillation，占"resolution-invariant distillation"，没占 lattice intervention。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4600:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3892:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/claude_review.md:23:- **L_adv** = GRL：disc 预测 variant slot，梯度反转使 z 不含可预测 lattice label。`slot=arange(M).repeat(B)` 与 image-major reshape 对齐。warmup-gated（adv_start），默认 lam_adv=0（弱辅助先不开，可后续 ablation）。✓
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4601:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3893:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/claude_review.md:29:- lattice 生成函数（make_lr/make_lattice_variants/pil_to_tensor_np/_to_target_aspect）**逐行复制自 cvpb_lattice_killswitch.py** → 训练降质与 GO kill-switch eval **byte-identical**（单变量隔离的关键）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4602:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3896:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_final.txt:8:- **High** [cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:173](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:173): per-sample RNG is reset from `seed + idx`, so each image gets the same height and same lattice variants every epoch. This contradicts design “每图每 iter 采样 M variants”.
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4603:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3897:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_final.txt:11:- **High** [cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:48](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:48), [cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:118](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:118): default `M=2` plus `mode = j % 3` trains only canonical + bbox-shift/bilinear. It does not train sub-pixel phase or zoom/antialias axes despite the method claim.
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4604:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3898:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_final.txt:12:  Fix: make real training default `M>=4` at minimum, or sample perturbation mode/kernel for each noncanonical variant; if keeping `L_adv`, store actual lattice labels.
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4606:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3900:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_final.txt:17:- **Medium** [cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:345](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:345): `L_id` triplet is computed over flattened `B*M`, not `mean_l Triplet(gf[:, l], y)`. This treats other variants of the same image as positives and is not exactly the stated per-lattice-slot formula.
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4607:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3901:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_final.txt:20:- **Low** [cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:96](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:96): lattice generation appears output-equivalent to kill-switch, but not literally byte-identical source.  
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4608:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:3902:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_final.txt:34:I did not find an exact prior for “sampling-lattice hidden variable / sub-pixel phase marginalization” in low-res ReID. Closest works model resolution gap or resolution-invariant features, not lattice phase marginalization: VPFA, LRAR, RFD; BlurPool covers general anti-aliasing/shift-invariance but not ReID lattice marginalization. Sources: [VPFA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00936), [LRAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2207.13037), [RFD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2109.07871), [BlurPool](https://arxiv.org/abs/1904.11486).

./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4657:experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_raw_v2.md:4059:低分辨率 ReID 的传统视角：LR = 模糊/缺细节，解法 = SR / resolution-invariant feature。**我们重定义**：一部分 LR 失败不是"缺信息"，而是 **采样格点不确定性（sampling-lattice uncertainty）**——同一个 HR 身份在不同合法的 LR 采样格点（sub-pixel phase / bbox alignment / downsample kernel）下，落到不同 embedding 区域，导致 rank-1 身份翻转。

./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_blc.md:1:# 实验 exp359-BLC: Bbox-Lattice Canonicalizer（训练端 input-level 备选）
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_blc.md:3:> **状态**：备选 design（2026-06-26 预写）。仅当 LSRC（对称 + 非对称）都 go/no-go 失败时启动。预写以备无缝接 + 体现"实验前先写 design"纪律。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_blc.md:7:- LSRC 走 backbone-loss 路线。codex train2_input 收敛到 **BLC（input-level，8/10→market 受限 6.5）**作互补/备选。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_blc.md:8:- **最对症的证据**：LM-S4 因子消融显示 **bbox 检测框不确定性主导（+2.84）**，远超 phase/kernel。→ 直接 canonicalize bbox 的 refiner 比改 resize kernel 更打中主因。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_blc.md:13:训一个 tiny crop refiner，把带 bbox/phase 扰动的 crop 重采样成 canonical crop，**削弱 bbox 主因子**，剩下的 phase/kernel/残差留给 test-time K=9 decision marginalization。目标**不是**让 K 个 lattice 特征一致（那是已证伪的 consistency），是把错误 crop 校正到更可靠的 person support。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_blc.md:19:  - `δ_z` 来自自生成的 bbox/phase lattice → 几何监督干净（压偏移参数，非身份 embedding）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_blc.md:22:- **⚠️ market 约束（codex 警告）**：market 图是已裁好的人框，无原图上下文 → 要先 **pad crops 人工制造 bbox 不确定性**，否则 refiner 没有可校正的 support 偏移。这是 BLC 在 market 上先天降到 6.5 的原因。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_blc.md:26:- 假设成立：offset MAE `<0.35` LR px，bbox-axis feature spread 降 ≥20%（identity margin 不降），K=1 mAP `+0.8` 或 K=9 在 lattice-marg 上再 `+0.3~0.5`。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_blc.md:31:- baseline：no-LM-loss（lattice 79.90 / single ~76.9）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_blc.md:37:冻 no-LM-loss backbone 只训 canonicalizer，h=12/16 bbox-only lattice：
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_blc.md:45:压几何偏移参数（非身份 embedding）→ 非 consistency；几何监督来自自生成 lattice（干净）→ 非弱监督瞎学；test 仍保留 K=9 marginalization → canonicalizer 只削 bbox 主因子，不替代边缘化。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:8:低分辨率 ReID 里，LR 观测只是 HR 场景在一个**未知采样格点**（sub-pixel phase / ±1 LR-pixel 检测框 crop / antialias kernel）下的一次采样；正确处理不是训练端逼特征不变，而是**检索时对格点隐变量做决策级边缘化**。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:13:- **因子分解（LM-S4）**: bbox 检测框 crop 不确定性主导（+2.84）> sub-pixel phase（+1.76）> zoom（+1.70）。→ mechanism = LR detector crop lattice，直观且新。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:16:1. **问题重定义** + 诊断: sampling lattice 是 LR-ReID 此前未显式建模的 test-time hidden nuisance；rank-level 干预证明它造成身份翻转。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:17:2. **方法（test-time）**: 决策级 lattice marginalization（K 变体 + mean/max/logsumexp 聚合），并证明 **decision-level marginalization > training-level invariance**。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:18:3. **方法（train-time，待定）**: A=LPA posterior 加权 / B=LC-STN canonicalization（廉价 kill-switch 验证中，过的进正文，否则进 discussion）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:21:- **LM-S2 inference 主实验**: lattice-marg 在 h=12/16/20/24/32 **全 beat 普通 TTA**，优势随分辨率单调递减（h12 +6.5 → h32 +0.5）= sampling-lattice 是 severe-LR nuisance 的干净证据；severe LR（h12/16）普通 TTA 反而有害。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:22:- **LM-S2-strong 防御**: 用更强 TTA（pad-crop + resize-jitter + color）当 baseline，lattice 仍赢且 gap 更大（h16 LATTICE−TTA +3.337）→ 堵死"不就是多裁几次"质疑。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:23:- **LM-S4 因子消融**: bbox 轴主导（见上）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:24:- **训练端反例（强消融卖点）**: consistency 拉特征到均值有害（−1.73 还掉 HR sanity）；lattice-aug 训练 ≈ 普通 random 增广（只 +0.54 不 lattice-specific，命门对照）；marginal-likelihood L_marg 是训练有害大元凶（cons-only 诊断）。→ 坐实"训练端 invariance/collapse 压判别性"，反衬 test-time marginalization。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:27:- **train-time A/B**: LPA oracle headroom（cheap kill-switch）→ LPA 学习头 OR LC-STN canonicalizer。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:28:- **Hard-Lattice ERM**（LM-S5，跑中）: 训练端 worst-case，codex 预测大概率负 → 进 discussion "为何 robust-ERM 也救不了训练端"。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:35:- TTA（k-reciprocal CVPR17 进 SOTA / FlipReID / BNTA）: test-time 后处理可发先例；我们是 lattice-specific marginalization 非通用 TTA。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:36:- Aliasing（BlurPool）: shift-variance 已知，但我们不是"发现 aliasing"，是"把 sampling lattice 当可边缘化隐变量"。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:37:- Alignment（STN/AlignedReID/STNReID/PAN/CDPM）: 若用 B LC-STN，卖点是"LR crop lattice 当隐变量 + 监督 canonicalization 非 invariance + 残差 marginalization"组合无先例。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/paper_skeleton.md:38:- Uncertainty（PFE/DUL/UMTS）: 若用 A LPA，卖点是 supervised sampling-lattice posterior 非 generic data-quality。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:1:# 实验 exp359-LSRC: Lattice-Set Retrieval Contrastive（训练端机制）
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:3:> ⚠️ **流程补记**：本 design 是**事后补写**（2026-06-26）。LSRC 已在 smoke 通过后直接启动全量训练，违反了"训练前先写 design + 双审查"铁律。现补 design + codex 独立审查（codex_lsrc_review.md），若审出 Critical 立即 kill 重跑。教训记入 memory `pre-experiment-review-discipline`。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:7:- test-time decision-marginalization（对 K=9 lattice 变体边缘化检索决策）已成立（6/10），但纯 test-time。用户要训练端互补机制凑成完整方法。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:8:- **frozen-feature 类训练端机制全部证负**：LS-MRT（set-retrieval 重投影 linear P）+0.028、LPA（query-side 加权）+0.075，两个冻结特征 probe 都 ≈0。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:9:- **诊断**：no-LM-loss 的冻结特征对 test-time 边缘化已近最优——lattice union 固定，重投影/重加权没有 headroom（oracle +4.338 是 gallery 真 ID 上界，frozen 够不着）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:10:- → 训练端价值必须**改特征本身（backbone）**。codex（train2_backbone）收敛到 LSRC，7.5/10。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:14:一句话：**训 backbone 让 K 个 lattice 变体形成"可边缘化的证据集"**——让负样本不产生假高 lattice 相似（marginalization 的数学瓶颈），让正样本有多路可匹配证据——比 frozen 重投影有空间，因为 backbone 能改变 aliasing/bbox-shift 敏感的特征本身。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:19:- **数据流**：B 张图各生成 M 个 lattice 变体 → backbone → `gf_bm [B,M,D]` → L2 norm → bag-to-bag `sim4 = einsum('ikd,jld->ijkl')` [B,B,M×M] cos → `S = tau_l·logsumexp(sim4/tau_l)` [B,B] set score。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:22:  - `L_negtail`：`softplus((max_neg_lattice_pair_cos - margin)/0.1)`，压低负样本对的最大 lattice-pair 相似度（核心：marg 最怕某错 lattice 假高分）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:23:  - `L_cov`（codex step2，默认关）：top-M 正样本 lattice-pair，正样本不靠单一固定 lattice 赢。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:24:- **关键超参**：lam_lsrc 0.5(4090)/1.0(3090) hedge，lsrc_tau 0.1，lsrc_margin 0.4，lr 1e-3，40 epoch，`--lam_marg 0 --lam_cons 0`（隔离 LSRC）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:28:- 假设成立：lattice mAP > no-LM-loss 79.90 **+0.3~0.5**，且 single_mean 不掉 >0.8（单变体判别力保住）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:29:- 失败最可能原因：(a) set-supcon 退化成普通 instance 判别，和 L_id 冗余（→ +0）；(b) neg-tail 压过头连正样本 lattice 也压（→ single 掉）；(c) 又一次"backbone 已最优、训练端无空间"。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:34:- **baseline**：no-LM-loss（exp359_abl_noLMloss）= 训练端不动、只 test-time 边缘化 → lattice 79.90 / single ~76.9。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:35:- **消融变量**：只开 LSRC（lam_marg=0, lam_cons=0），单变量隔离 LSRC 贡献。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/design_lsrc.md:42:- 非 **Hard-Lattice ERM**（76.894 死）：把 K 变体当检索证据集，非独立硬样本逐个 ERM。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_final.txt:8:- **High** [cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:173](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:173): per-sample RNG is reset from `seed + idx`, so each image gets the same height and same lattice variants every epoch. This contradicts design “每图每 iter 采样 M variants”.
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_final.txt:11:- **High** [cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:48](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:48), [cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:118](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:118): default `M=2` plus `mode = j % 3` trains only canonical + bbox-shift/bilinear. It does not train sub-pixel phase or zoom/antialias axes despite the method claim.
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_final.txt:12:  Fix: make real training default `M>=4` at minimum, or sample perturbation mode/kernel for each noncanonical variant; if keeping `L_adv`, store actual lattice labels.
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_final.txt:17:- **Medium** [cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:345](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:345): `L_id` triplet is computed over flattened `B*M`, not `mean_l Triplet(gf[:, l], y)`. This treats other variants of the same image as positives and is not exactly the stated per-lattice-slot formula.
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_final.txt:20:- **Low** [cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:96](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/cvpb_lm_reid_train.py:96): lattice generation appears output-equivalent to kill-switch, but not literally byte-identical source.  
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/codex_review_final.txt:34:I did not find an exact prior for “sampling-lattice hidden variable / sub-pixel phase marginalization” in low-res ReID. Closest works model resolution gap or resolution-invariant features, not lattice phase marginalization: VPFA, LRAR, RFD; BlurPool covers general anti-aliasing/shift-invariance but not ReID lattice marginalization. Sources: [VPFA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00936), [LRAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2207.13037), [RFD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2109.07871), [BlurPool](https://arxiv.org/abs/1904.11486).
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:1:# LM-ReID 训练端创新 pipeline（2026-06-26，用户指令"训练端不许停"后）
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:3:## ★更新（2026-06-26 凌晨）：frozen-feature 类全死 → 转 LSRC（改 backbone）
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:5:- **LS-MRT 死**：冻 backbone probe，smoke 时 P(D×D linear ~1M params)过拟合暴跌 −8.694；修复（identity-reg + 降 lr + 全量 116k samples）后 **+0.028 clean FAIL**（K-cosine 不升，但 P 不帮忙）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:6:- **LPA 死**：query-side 加权 +0.075，预测最佳变体 acc 12.4%≈chance。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:7:- **关键发现**：LS-MRT(+0.028)+LPA(+0.075) 两个冻结特征 probe 都 ~0 → **no-LM-loss 特征对 test-time 边缘化已近最优，重投影/重加权救不了**（oracle +4.338 是 gallery 真 ID 上界，frozen-feature 够不着）。**LCRS/LRFD/DeepSets 也是 frozen-feature 重投影，大概率同样死**。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:12:| **LSRC** | 7.5/10 | full fine-tune，`L_id + lam_lsrc*(bag-to-bag set-supcon logsumexp + **neg-tail 压负样本假高 lattice**)`。打 marginalization 数学瓶颈（更少假高负例+更多可复用正证据），**解释了 frozen 为何没空间**（lattice union 已固定） | 不依赖原图 ✓ | **进行中** 4090 lam0.5 + 3090 lam1.0，acc 0.98/0.97 健康，判据 lattice mAP > 79.90 +0.3 且 single 不掉 |
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:13:| BLC/LC-STN++ | 8/10→6.5 | bbox crop refiner 改输入（最贴 bbox 主导发现） | market 只裁好框→6.5 | 备选 |
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:16:## （旧）5 候选 frozen-feature pipeline（LS-MRT 已死，其余大概率同死）
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:18:LPA(A 加权头)定死（oracle headroom +4.338 不可达，最佳变体 query+gallery 共定单看 query 预测不出 acc≈chance）。Hard-Lattice ERM eval 中。按用户"这三个不行就找更多"启 4 新 codex（litreview2/train_more_*.md），收敛到 **5 个候选**，全避开已死的（invariance-collapse / query-side 预测最佳变体 / L_marg 分类头边缘化）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:22:| **LS-MRT**（set-wise 检索） | 7/10 | 把 test-time 边缘化写进**训练检索损失**：`S=logmeanexp_k sim(z_q,k, z_g)`，supervised contrastive over gallery。**在检索决策层边缘化非分类头**——直接修 L_marg 失败因（L_marg 在 train-ID classifier posterior 求均值→塌缩；LS-MRT 在 q-g 相似度证据边缘化，denominator 有真负 gallery） | 冻 backbone + cached K=9 features 训小 P(linear/BNNeck+τ)，**最廉价**；活=h12/16 ≥+0.3 且 K-cosine 不升 | **先跑** |
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:23:| LCRS（互补 residual） | 7/10 | `z_k = norm(P_shared(g_k) + α·P_k(g_k))`，shared identity core + lattice-specific residual subspace + decorr(只在分类正确后)。"准确后分工"非硬推开。test K=9 边缘化拿更富 union | 冻 backbone 训 P_shared/P_k/cls；活=K-error correlation 降+K=9 gain ≥+0.5，individual variant mAP 不掉>0.8 | 排队 |
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:24:| LRFD（disentangle） | 7/10 | `z_l=P_id, r_l=P_lat`(lattice nuisance sink, 必须能预测 lattice), per-variant CE+triplet, 推理丢 r_l 用 z_l 边缘化 | 冻 backbone 训 heads；活=with-lattice-code >+0.2 over without + r_l lattice-pred acc>60% | 排队 |
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:25:| LC-STN（对齐 canonicalize） | 7/10 | tiny localization net 预测 bbox offset(dx,dy)+grid_sample 重采样到 canonical, L_geo=SmoothL1 监督几何非身份, 残差留 marginalization | 冻 backbone 训 canonicalizer；活=offset MAE<0.35 LR px + K=1 +0.8 OR K=9 +0.3 | 排队 |
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:26:| DeepSets Marginalizer（LPA 修正） | 6.5/10 | 不预测 query 最佳变体(LPA 死因)，改 pairwise: 每个(q,g)的 K 个 cos 经 DeepSets φ/ρ 合成校准分, α 初 0 限幅, embedding 冻 | cached features 小 scorer；活=+0.4 over uniform, 且 ordinary-TTA scorer 追不平(否则只是 generic learned TTA) | 排队 |
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:28:**不做**（codex 共判）：TTT/TENT(无标签自我确认 3/10), DRO/Hard-Lattice(≈将死 4/10), PFE-Gaussian(query-side uncertainty 不够 4/10)。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/train_time_pipeline.md:30:**执行顺序**：LS-MRT 冻 probe 先（最廉价+最强故事）→ 活则全量；死则 LCRS→LRFD→LC-STN→DeepSets 逐个 cheap probe。**任何一个过线即训练端第二 contribution，凑成 train+test 完整方法（→7-8/10）。全死也不停——再启 codex 找更多。** novelty 共识：卖点不是各机制本身(decorr/disentangle/STN/DeepSets 都老)，是"**LR 采样格点当可枚举隐变量 + 训练端 X + 测试端 decision marginalization**"这个 problem reformulation + train/test 对齐。
./experiments/exp324/design.md:11:- **我们的咬合**：MaxSim 天然吃 DINO dense tokens（late-interaction，非全局池化）；pose+5-part 把 DINO 在低分辨率人物上 noisy 的 correspondence 约束到 body-part 语义上降噪；只比 mutually-visible part 对症遮挡。24G 最稳、不依赖大模型、最复用现成 MaxSim 全套。
./experiments/exp324/design.md:28:- 失败最可能原因：(1) DINO correspondence 在 128×256 低分辨率脏 crop 上漂移严重，pose 降噪不够；(2) part-MaxSim 不带训练直接 eval 太弱（DINO 特征非 ReID-judiciable）；(3) 只在整体涨、重遮挡组不涨。任一即降级。
./experiments/exp324b/_codex_review.log:1086:scripts/exp323_crop.py:20:    marked visible (visibility_binary) and have valid coords; take their bbox + 15% pad.
./experiments/exp324b/_codex_review.log:1140:experiments/decisions.md:3746:- 3 splits .npz **全部 7 字段齐**(heatmap/keypoints/scores/bbox/crop_bounds/visibility/visibility_binary)
./experiments/exp324b/_codex_review.log:1166:experiments/exp323/design.md:35:- 失败最可能原因：(1) 低分辨率监控行人图上 MLLM 判定噪声大，A/B 都烂；(2) pose grounding 不改变判定（MLLM 不听 grounding 或本就用可见部位）；(3) 增益均匀分布（→ 通用重排，撞红线#6）。任一即砍，转 exp324。

./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:41:**诚实分解**：①+27 大头是 LR fine-tune（标准），②lattice-marg 真贡献 +2.63 但 robust beat TTA +3.504（frozen +3.04 / trained +3.504 都成立）。**HR-gallery 退化担心是多虑**（SANITY 掉但 LR mAP 高=模型学了 LR↔HR 对应，牺牲 HR-HR 换 LR-HR 正是 CR-ReID 目标，已纠正误判）。codex 过线被远超**但大头是 LR fine-tune 不是 lattice 机制** → 真判据靠 ablation。

./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:47:- 判：LM-ReID single > ablation single → consistency 训练有独立价值（强 method）；≈ → 只是"LR 增强 + 测试时 lattice ensemble"（弱，test-time trick 级）。
./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:53:| h | single | lattice-mean | lattice-MaxSim | TTA-MaxSim | **LATTICE−TTA** |

./experiments/exp359_lm_reid/monitor.md:60:- **诚实**：大头是 LR fine-tune（single 75.71 vs frozen single 42.65=+33，标准 CR-ReID，非创新）；**lattice-marg 真贡献 +2.33（robust，frozen +3.04/trained +3.006 一致 beat TTA）= 方法的 lattice-specific 贡献，但是 test-time-trick 量级**。
